5. jñānapaṭalaḥ pañcamaḥ (1) yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ ||
namaḥ śrīkālacakāya ||
yenākṛṣya manobhavaḥ svakuliśānnīto lalāṭaṃ svakaṃ prajñājñānabalena śākyamuninā vajraṃ mahoṣṇīṣakam |
sālambā'naṇuśūnyatā sukaruṇā'nālambinī yasya ve tasmai devanarāsurāhigurave viśvekaśāstre namaḥ ||
kāraṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ nāsti calo nāsti kramastathā |
kālacakrābhidhānena yasya natvā tamadvayam ||
nirmāṇasambhogasudharmaśuddhaṃ kādyakṣare: kāyacatuṣkamuktam |
yasyādibuddhasya niranvayasya tathāgataṃ taṃ praṇipatya mūrdhnā ||
śrījñānapaṭale ṭīkā mañjuśrīcoditena vai |
likhyate puṇḍarīkeṇa sugatavyākṛtena ca ||
mayā nirmitakāyena lokeśenābjadhāriṇā |
ṛṣīṇāṃ sarvasattvānāṃ puṇyajñānaphalāptaye ||
iha śrīmati kalāpagrāmadakṣiṇamalayodyāne śrīkālacakramaṇḍala gṛhapūrvadvārāvasāne ratnamaṇḍape mahāmaṇiratnasiṃhāsanastho yaśonarendro mañjuśrīnirmitakāyo brahmarṣisūrya- rathādhyeṣitaḥ paramādibuddhājjñānapaṭale sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ tathāgataprativacanaṃ prathamavṛttenāha- kiñcijjñātaṃ hi bhartujinavarasahitaṃ sādhanaṃ yattvayoktaṃ bhūyo'haṃ śrotukāmastridaśanaraguro maṇḍalaṃ dhātubhedaiḥ buddhānāṃ ṣaṭkulāni tvalikaliṣu gatānyeva ṣaḍ yoginīnāṃ śrutvā saucandravākyaṃ gadati jinapati rdhātubhimaṇḍalādyam ||
1||
iha prathamavṛttenādibuddhāt sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ tathāgataprativacanamarthaśaraṇatayā saṃgītaṃ mañjuśriyā, tadeva ṭīkayā vitanyate - kiñcidityādi |
iha 'sādhanapaṭale yadbhartuḥ sādhanaṃ tathāgatasahitaṃ kālacakrasya yat tvayoktaṃ he tridaśanaraguro śākya 'mune !
tatkiñcit mayā jñātam |
tasmād bhūyo'haṃ śrotukāmaḥ kālacakra maṇḍalaṃ śarīre dhātubhedairiti, asthyādi- dhātuviśuddhibhiriti |
anyacca buddhānāṃ ṣaṭkulāni śrotukāmaḥ, yā e nyalika liṣu gatānīti, akārādi svareṣu gatāni, kakārādivyañjaneṣu gatānīti vakṣya- māṇāni, evaṃ ṣaḍ yoginīnāmiti |
yathā ṣaṭskandhānāṃ ṣaṭ kulāni tathā ṣaḍdhātūnāmapi ādikādiṣu gatānīti |
śrutvā saucandravākyamidaṃ tato gardāti jinapatiḥ sahajādicatu:- kāyātmanirāvaraṇaskandhā [daya]:, teṣāṃ patirjanakaḥ saṃbuddha ādibuddha iti |
dhātu- bhimaṃṇḍalādyamiti ṣaṭkulādikaṃ vakṣyamāṇa [m ] |
iti deśakādhyeṣakasambandhaḥ |
asmin vṛtte pādatrayeṇa sucandrādhyeṣaṇam, caturthapādamārabhya yāvat paṭalaparisamāptistāvat tathā- gataprativacanam |
punaradhyeṣaṇā nāsti [iti] asmin tantre niyamaḥ ||
1 ||
idānīṃ dhātuviśuddhayā maṇḍalaviśuddhirucyate- stambhān vajrāvalīṃ vai kuru mahivalayaṃ bhittimevāsthibhiśca māṃsāsṛktoyavibhiḥ surayamadhanade cāpare raṅgapātam |
pittena śleṣmaṇākaṃ śaśinamapi tathā snāyubhistoyajāni pañcaprākārarekhāḥ kṣitija lahutabhuṅamārutākāśajābhiḥ ||
2||
stambhānityādi |
iha śarīrāsthibhiḥ śarīre kāyamaṇḍale stambhān kuru iti kalpanayā |
evaṃ vajrāvaloṃ pṛthvīvalayaṃ bhittimapi, asthibhiḥ kurviti vṛttatraye sambandhaḥ |
anayā viśuddhayā'sthicūrṇādikaṃ prakṣiped rajomadhye |
māṃsāsṛktoya vibhiriti, māṃsaviśuddhayā sure pūrve raṅgapātaḥ, raktaviśuddhayā yame dakṣiṇe raṅgapātaḥ toyaviśuddhayā uttare dhanade raṅgamātaḥ, viviśuddhayā'pare paścime raṅgapāta ' iti |
madhye śukraviśuddhayā raṅgapātaḥ |
yatra ṣaṣṭho rajastatra rajoviśuddhayā raṅgapāta iti |
evaṃ tathāgatamāṃsādiviśuddhayā yathākramamamogho ratneśo'mitābho vairocano'kṣobhyo vajrasattvaśceti ṣaṭ |
pitteneti pittadhātunā arkāsanāni śleṣmadhātunā candrāsanānīti |
akaṃ śaśinamapi |
snāyubhistoyajāni candrādityāsanatale |
pañcaprākārarekhā iti |
prathamarekhā kṣitijayā aṅguṣṭhaviśuddhayā, dvitīyā toṣajayā tarjanīviśuddhayā, tṛtīyā vahnijayā madhyamā- viśuddhayā, caturthī vāyujayā anāmikā viśuddhayā, pañcamyākāśajayā kaniṣṭhā- viśuddhayeti |
evaṃ pañcaprākārarekhāḥ kuru rajomaṇḍale iti ||
2 ||
yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ bhartrabjaṃ kālanāḍyā calaśikhivalayādyaṃ ca carmādibhiśca arkadvārāṇi randhrermaṇimayaracanāṃ dantapaṅktyā tathaiva |
cakrāṇyaṣṭī śmaśāne calavalayagatānyaṅgulīnāṃ nakhaiśca vajrācilamabhirvai diśividiśigatai maṃṇḍalasyāvasāne ||
3 |
bhabjaṃ kālanāḍyeti |
avadhūtyā nāyakasya padmaṃ viśuddham, śeṣāṇi lalanādibhiriti dvāsaptatisahasraparyantaiḥ snāyubhiriti niyamaḥ |
calaśikhivalayādyaṃ carmādibhiśceti |
iha vāyuvalayaṃ carmabhiḥ, agnivalayaṃ 'raktoṣṇadhātunā udakavalayaṃ prasvedena |
akaṃdvārāṇīti dvādaśadvārāṇi dvādaśarandhraḥ |
śrotre dve, ghrāṇaṃ dve, netre dve, mukhe ekam, mūtraviṭ- śukrarandhrāṇi trīṇi, stanadvaye dve - evaṃ dvādaśa randhrāṇi rajomaṇḍale kuru dvādaśarandhra- viśuddhayeti |
maṇimayaracanā yā maṇḍale hārādikā yā (tāṃ) dantapaṅktyā kuru |
cakrā- vyaṣṭau śmaśāne yāni tānyaṅgulonāṃ nakhaiśceti hastapādanakhaviśuddhayā kuru |
vajrā- cilamabhirvai iti |
bāhyavajrāvalīṃ vajrajvālā lomabhiḥ kuru |
diśiviviśigatairlomabhi- maṃṇḍalasyāvasāne vajrāciḥ ||
3 ||
evaṃ vai kāyavajraṃ varavividhaguṇaṃ maṇḍalākāramuktaṃ vāgvajraṃ cādikādyaṃ sakalajina kulairdevatākārameva |
ṣaṭśūnyaiścittavatraṃ trividhabhavagataṃ nāyakākārameka- mevaṃ vai jñānavatraṃ bhavabhayamathanaṃ viśvamātṛsvarūpam ||
4||
evaṃ vai kāyavajñaṃ ṣaḍdhātvātmakam, varavividhaguṇaṃ pṛthivyādibhedaiḥ, maṇḍalākāra- muktamiti maṇḍalaniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ devatāgaṇaviśuddhirucyate – vāgvajyamityādi |
ihā- dikādyaṃ svaravyañjanātmakam, vāgvatraṃ svaravyañjanapariṇataṃ devatāgaṇam, sakala- jinakulaiḥ sārdham |
tena vāgvajraṃ devatākāramevoktam |
tadeva rajomaṇḍale devatākāraṃ kuru iti |
ṣaṭśūnyaiścittavajjramiti cakṣuvijñānaṃ śrotravijñānaṃ ghrāṇavijñānaṃ jihvāvijñānaṃ kāyavijñānaṃ manovijñānaṃ viśuddhayā ṣaḍvidham |
ṣaṭśūnyaṃ rūpādiviṣayainirāvaraṇaṃ cittavajjaṃ bhavati |
trividha' bhavagataṃ sarvavyāpi nāyakākāramekaṃ kuru |
evaṃ ve jñānavāṃ grāhyacittaṃ trividhabhavagataṃ viśvamātusvarūpaṃ śūnyatābimbaṃ bhavabhayamathanaṃ sarvakalpanā- vinirmuktam |
prajñāṃ kuru maṇḍale nāyikāmiti |
evaṃ śarīre kāyavāścittajñānadhātavaḥ, bāhye maṇḍalaṃ devatāgaṇo nāyako nāyikā ceti saṃvṛtyā, vivṛtyā nirmāṇaṃ sambhogaṃ dharmaṃ sahajamiti siddham ||
4 ||
ga. raktakṛṣṇa |
ca. guda |
ca viśuddhaṃ |
ga. bhagavatā |
ga. grāhyaṃ |
ga. kuru iti |
evaṃ ṣaṭśūnyaiścittavajñamiti |
idānīṃ ṣaṇmantrakulānyucyante- vimalaprabhāyāṃ vargāṇāṃ kādiṣaṇṇāṃ kramapariracitānāṃ kulāni krameṇa vajrāsī ratnacakaṃ jalajamapi tathā kartikā vai jinānām |
bhūyaścaikaikavargaḥ pratyekaṃ sakalajinavaraibhidyate pañcabhedaiḥ varṇabhedāt kṣitijalahutabhuṅmārutākāśabhāvāt ||
5 ||
[ jñāna- vargāṇāmityādi |
iha kādayaḥ ṣaḍvargāḥ ka ca ṭa ta pa 'śā iti, teṣāṃ vargāṇāṃ kāviṣaṇṇāṃ kramapariracitānāṃ kulāni ṣaḍ jinānāṃ vajrādicihnānīti |
tatra vajraṃ kavargakulam asiścavargakulaṃ, ratnaṃ ṭavargakulaṃ cakraṃ tavargakulaṃ jalajamapi pavargakulaṃ tathā katikā 'śavargakulam, ve ekāntamiti vargakulaniyamaḥ |
bhūyaścaikaikavargaḥ sakalajina- varaibhidyate pañcabhederiti |
iha yatraikaṃ tatra pañcakulāni samudayasatyena bhavanti |
tena tairvyavahāro laukika siddhaye |
ataścaikaiko varga: pratyekaṃ varṇabhedāt kṣitibhāvājjala-
bhāvād hutabhugbhāvānmārutabhāvādākāśabhāvād bhidyate pañcadhā vargaḥ
pratyeka miti ka kha ga gha ṅa iti || 5 ||
evaṃ cādayaḥ saṃhārakrameṇa paṭhitāḥ pratyāhārapāṭhena punadayaḥ sṛṣṭikrameṇā- kāśādinā paṭhitā iti te'yucyante-
ādī śūnyaprabhedāsvapi ṅa ña ṇa ma nākastathādisvaraśca vāyorbhedastvikārastu gha jha ḍha bha dha śā vahnibhedāstvagādyāḥ
ambhobhedo'pyukāraḥ - pakha chaṭha phatha vā bhūmibhede lṛkādyā evaṃ pañcaprakāre paramajinakule: kādivargā vibhinnāḥ || 6 ||
ādāvityādi

evaṃ vāyorbhedastvikāro gha ḍha bha dha śā yakāraśceti
vahneḥ ṛ ga ja ḍa ba da ṣā ra iti, toyasya ukha chaṭha phatha pāva iti, bhūmeḥ la ka ca ṭa pa ta sāla iti
evaṃ pañcaprakāraiḥ paramajitakulaiḥ kādivargā vibhinnā iti vajrakhaṅga ratnapadmacakrakulairuktākṣarāṇi ṅādīni veditavyānīti niyamaḥ || 6 ||
idānīmuṣṇīṣakulānyucyante- uṣṇīṣaṃ pañcaśūnyaṃ trikulamapi tathā kāyavākcittavajra- mādyā mudrākulaṃ vai hayaravalayutāḥ kādivargāḥ samastāḥ

uṣṇīṣamityādi

eṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaditi ṣaṭtriṃśa- tantreṣu cakranāthā api bhavanti ṣaṭtriṃśadvyañjanasthānasvarūpeṇeti prathamaṃ kavyañjana- nāyakena kavajtratantramucyate

yoginīyogatantram ||9||
mātrāsaṃkhyātmako vai tridaśanavadiśābhiśca ṣaṭtriṃśadātmā yādyābhirdvārapālāḥ sakalagaṇakulaṃ śeṣamātrābhireva 1 ṣaṭkrodhā hādibhiḥ syuḥ svakuladiśigatā vāyumātrādibhedeḥ pratyekaṃ varṇabhede rasaguṇitarasaṃ mātrā saṃkhyātmako vai tridaśanavadiśābhiśceti
tatra trayodaśabhistrayodaśātmā pūrvokta: kya kra kva kla ebhiryaṇādeśairyuktastrayodaśātmā dvārapālaiḥ sahāpyasī punarguṇairbhinna ekonaviṃśadātmā bhavati ka ke kara ko kala kamityebhiḥ saha
tataḥ pañcaviṃśadātmā guṇavṛddhibhiḥ saha kā ke kār ko kāl kaḥ iti
tato dvātriṃśadātmā vahā kyā kā kvā klā kahā : iti garbhaprajñayā saha
tataścatustriśadātmā kaṃ kaḥ sahitaḥ
evaṃ ṣaṭ - triśadātmā kahaṃ vaha: sahita iti sarvatra saptatriṃśadātmako madhye hūṃkāro nāyakaḥ
ṣaṭ- triṃśanmāṇḍaleyāḥ ka kā ki kī kṛ kṝ ku kū klū klū kaṃ kaḥ iti
hrasvadīrghasvarabhinno varṇaḥ
tathā ka kā ke ke kar kāra ko ko kal kāla kaṃ kaḥ iti guṇavṛddhibhirbhinno 'varṇa:
vaha kahā kya kyā kra kā kva kvā kla klā kahaṃ khaḥ iti hrasvadīrghayaṇādeśa- bhinno varṇaḥ kakāraḥ
khādayo'pi pratyekaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaditi
evaṃ khavajjādikaṃ tantramucyate kavyañjanātmakaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmātrābhinnamiti
evaṃ ṣaśivātmā maṇḍaleśaḥ
yādyābhi- dvārapālā iti yaṇādeśasahitairvyañjanerdvārapālāḥ 'kya kra kva kla ityebhiḥ
sakala- gaṇakulaṃ skandhadhātvāyatanalakṣaṇam
śeṣaiḥ svarairhrasvadīrghaguṇavṛddhisthānīyairiti
śeṣa- mātrābhireva ṣaṭkrodhā hādibhiḥ syuriti
yatra daśa krodhā daśadikṣu bhavanti, tatrāpare ṣaṭkodhāste hādibhirdīrgheryaṇādeśaḥ syuḥ vaha vahā kā kvā klā iti
yatra krodhendrastatra vaha vahaḥ iti dvādaśakrodhāḥ ṣaṭ kulatantra, ekakulatantre'pi ṣaṇmātrākulabhedairiti niyamaḥ
'evamākāśadhāturūrdhve svakulaviśigato jñānadhāturadhogataḥ
apare vāyumātrādibhedaiḥ pūrve dakṣiṇottare paścime gatā devatā iti
evaṃ pratyekaṃ vyañjanaṃ kakārādikaṃ varṇabhedaiḥ svarabhedaiḥ, rasaguṇitarasamiti ṣaṭtriṃśadbhedabhinnam, yoginoyogatantramiti yatra yoginīnāṃ sañcāro nāyako niścalaḥ saṃvṛtyā tadyoginītantram, yatropāyasya sañcāraḥ prajñā niścalā tadupāyatantram
svarūpataḥ sarvameva prajñopāyātmakaṃ yogatantram
tathāha heva - hekāreṇa mahākaruṇā vajraṃ prajñeti bhaṇyate
prajñopāyātmakaṃ tantraṃ tanme nigaditaṃ śṛṇu || ki
itaḥ paraṃ punaḥ prārabhyate

samājaṃ mīlanaṃ proktaṃ sarvabuddhābhidhānakam || iti vacanāt nedamupāyatantraṃ bhavati
tathāvibuddhe- yogo nopāyakāye na ekayā prajñayā bhavet
prajñopāyasamāpattiryoga uktastathāgataiḥ || iti || 9 ||
evaṃ pratyekavarṇo rasaguṇitarase maṇḍale maṇḍaleśa: skandhairbhūtendriyādyaiḥ sakalagaṇakulaṃ śodhayenmātṛbhedaiḥ
śūnyaścānāhatādyaiḥ sakalajinakulairmudraṇaṃ devatānā- muṣṇīṣādambujānte viṣamasamakule cakramadhyāsane ca || 10 ||
evaṃ pratyekavarṇo rasaguṇitarasa iti ṣaṭtriṃśadyoginīyogatantre maṇḍale maṇḍaleśa iti siddhamekakulatantraṃ trikulaṃ pañcakulaṃ yattat tadeva sahasralakṣa koṭibhedabhinnam
tantrāṇāṃ saṃkhyā nāsti, samājādīnāṃ hevajrādīnāmanantasattvāśayavaśāditi tantraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ mantradevatānāmadhyātmaśuddhirucyate - skandhairityādi
iha pañcaskandhaiḥ pañcātmā śuddhayati, skandhapañcabhūtairnavātmā śuddhayati, catuḥ karmendriyaiḥ saha
trayodaśātmā śuddhayati, ' ṣaḍindriyaiḥ saha ekonaviṃśadātmā śuddhayati, ṣaḍ- viṣayaiḥ saha pañcaviśadātmā śuddhayati, pañcakarmendriyakriyābhiḥ saha pañca- karmendriye praviṣṭe svābhaprajñayā saha dvātriṃśa dātmā, ṣaṣṭhakarmendriya- ' ṣaṣṭhaka- rmendrikriyāyuktaścatustriśadātmā, ṣaṭskandhadhātubhyāṃ yuktaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadātmā iti
evaṃ ṣaṭskandhāḥ ṣaḍdhātavaḥ ṣaḍindriyāṇi ṣaḍviṣayāḥ ṣaṭkarmendriyāṇi ṣaṭ karmendriyakriyā iti dhātavo māṇḍaleyāḥ

tathā ka kā ke kai kar kār ko kau kal kāl kaṃ kaḥ iti dvādaśāyatanāni siddhāni
eṣāṃ dhātūnāṃ nāyakaḥ pañcākṣarī mahāśūnya upāyo binduśūnya: ṣaḍakṣara: prajñā vaṃkāra ekāra ityevaṃkāro nāyako mahāsukho maṇḍalādhipati - ryathā kavajñatantre tathā khavajrādike siddhaḥ
evaṃ skandhadhātavo garbhapuṭe dvādaśāyatanāni bāhyapuṭe dvārapālāḥ karmendriyāṇi saviṣayāṇīti
iha yathā kumāraḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣādūrdhva- mīśvaro'nantacakrīti putraduhitṛskandhānāṃ janakatvāt, tathā pañcatathāgatā divyūhaspharaṇā- ddevatāgaṇanāyaka īśvaro'nantacakrī bhavatīti niyamaḥ
iha yathā garbhasya kāyaniṣpattistathā devatāniṣpattau maṇḍala rājāgrī sevāṅgam, yathā vāniṣpattistathā karmarājāyupa- sādhanam, yathā bodhicittabindu niṣpattistathā binduyoga: sādhanam, yathā śukracyuto sukhotpattistathā sūkṣmayogo mahāsādhanam
evaṃ caturaṅgasādhanamadhyātmapaṭale
prāgukte śodhanīyaṃ vistarata iti niyamaḥ || 12 ||
idānīṃ ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaḍdhātubhyaḥ kuladevatāspharaṇamucyate- śuddhe saṃśuddhatoyācchaśadharadhavala: padmacihno'mitābho mānī lokeśvaro ve hayarathagamanā māmakī rūpavajrā |
raudrī cābdhirgaṇendro dvijaphaṇisahito vyāghravaktrā salūkā ete śrīvāmavaktrāt paramakaruṇayā sphāritā vajriṇā ca || 13 ||
śuddha ityādi |
'iha ṣoḍaśavarṣādūdhvaṃ yathā manuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaḍdhātusvabhāvena cittasya nānāspharaṇam, tathā ṣaḍdhātubhedena nāyakasyāpi |
śuddha iti lalāṭacakre saṃśuddhatoyā- viti nirāvaraṇatoyadhātoḥ saṃjñāskandhato'pi devatāgaṇa: sphurati śaśadharadhavalaḥ padmacihnaḥ tadyathā amitābhaḥ |
māmakī padmāntakaḥ acala: mānī cundā lokeśvaraḥ rūpavajrā raudrī lakṣmī samudro gaṇendraḥ ananto nāgaḥ kuliko vyāghravaktrā ulūkavaktrā ityetā devatāḥ paramakaruṇayā sphāritā vāmavaktrāditi kāyavaktrācchuklāt |
kālacakrabhagavato niyamaḥ |
atrāmitābho vijñānamudrito devatāgaṇo'mitābhena padma- cihnaḥ śāntipuṣṭikarmasiddhida iti śuklatoyavarṇataḥ |
evamamita bhakuladevatāspharaṇaṃ saṃharaṇaṃ ca pratyavekṣaṇata iti || 13 ||
idānīmamoghasiddhikulaspharaṇamucyate- dharme saṃśuddhavāyostvasikarakamalo'moghasiddhiśca tārā vaigarbhaḥ sparśavatrā tvatibalasahitā carcikā śṛṅkhalā ca |
paṭale, 12-16 ślo. ]
yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ naiṛtyaḥ śūdranāgāḥ pavana iti tathā śvānavaktrā sakākā maitrīcittena caite tribhuvanapatinā sphāritā pūrvavaktrāt || 14 ||
dharma ityādi |
iha hṛdaye dharmacakre saṃśuddhavāyudhātoḥ śuddhasaṃskārāt khaṅgacihno devatāgaṇa: kṛṣṇavarṇataḥ |
tadyathā amoghasiddhistārā vighnāntako nīladaṇḍo'tibalā vajraśṛṅkhalā khagarbhaḥ sparśavatrā cacikā vaiṣṇavī vāyunairṛtyaḥ padmaḥ karkoṭaka: kākāsyā śvānāsyā etā: [ 172b ] pūrvavaktrāditi cittavaktrāt |
maitrīcittena sphāritāstribhuvanapatineti |
atrāmoghasiddhivijñānena mudrito devatāgaṇo'moghasiddhinā khaḍgacihna uccāṭanavidveṣa karmasiddhidaḥ 'kṛṣṇo vāyuvarṇataḥ |
evamamoghasiddhikuladevatānāṃ spharaṇaṃ saṃharaṇaṃ ca kṛtyānuṣṭhānata iti || 14 ||
idānīṃ ratnasambhavakulaspharaṇamucyate- sambhoge śuddhavahnerudayaravinibho ratnadhṛk pāṇḍarā ca bhūgarbho jambhako ve vararasakuliśā śūkarī ṣaṇmukhāgniḥ |
rājāno dvo phaṇīndrau prakaṭitabhṛkuṭī śūkarāsyā sagṛdhrā ete śrī savyavaktrāt paramamuditayā sphāritā viśvabhartrā || 15 ||
saṃbhoge śuddhavahneriti |
iha kaṇṭhacakre śuddhavedanāyā śuddhavarudayara vinibha iti raktavarṇo ratnaghṛgiti ratnasambhavaḥ |
evaṃ pāṇḍarā |
prajñāntakaḥ ṭakkirājo jambhī bhṛkuṭī kṣitigarbho rasavatrā vārāhī kaumārī agniḥ kārtikeyaḥ śukarāsyā gudhavaktrā vāsukiḥ śaṅkhapāla ete śrīsavyavaktrāt paramamuditayā sphāritā viśvabhartrā iti |
sarve raktavarṇā ratnasaṃbhava mudritā vaśyākṛṣṭisiddhidā vākcakrasvabhāvataḥ |
evaṃ ratnasaṃ- bhavakuladevatānāṃ spharaṇādikaṃ samatājñānata iti |
atrāpi ratnasambhavo vijñāna- mudrita iti || 15 ||
idānīṃ vairocana kuladevatāspharaṇamucyate- nābhī saṃśuddhabhūmerva ra kanakanibhaścakrapāṇirmuniśca viṣkambhī locanā ca pravaranarapate stambhako gandhavatrā |
mārīcyendrī ca śakro yugamukhasahito vaiśyanāgādi sarve bhartrā copekṣayā ve sakalajanahitāḥ sphāritāḥ paścimāsyāt || 16 ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- nābhau saṃśuddhabhūmerityādi |
iha nābhāviti nirmāṇacakre śuddharūpaskandhāt śuddha- pṛthvīdhātovara kanakanibhaḥ pītavarṇaścakrapāṇirmuniśceti vairocanaḥ |
evaṃ locanā |
yamāntako mahābalaḥ stambhī mārīcī viṣkambhī gandhavatrā aindrī brahmāṇī śakro brahmā takṣako mahāpadmo jambukāsthā garuḍāsyā etā bhartrā copekṣayā ve sakalajanahitāḥ sphāritā: paścimāsyāditi |
jñānavaktrāt pītavarṇāścakracihnāḥ ' stambhana mohanādi - karmasiddhidā vairocanamudritāḥ |
vairocano'pi vijñānamudritaḥ |
evaṃ vairocana kulaspharaṇā- dikamādarśajñānata iti || 16 ||
idānīmakṣobhya kulaspharaṇamucyate- uṣṇīṣe śuddhaśūnyād varakuliśadharo'kṣobhyadhātvīśvarī dve uṣṇīṣo dharmadhāturgagana guṇavaśād vajrapāṇyādayo'nye |
guhye śuddhāt samastāḥ śamasukhanidhayaḥ śabdavajrādayaśca ūrdhvādhaḥ sphāritā ve bahuguṇanilayāḥ prāṇināṃ mokṣahetoḥ || 17 ||
uṣṇīṣe śuddhaśūnyāditi | uṣṇīṣacakre śuddhavijñānaskandhāt śuddhākāśadhātoḥ, varakuliśadharo'kṣobhyo jñānamudritaḥ śyāmavarṇaḥ |
evaṃ vajradhātvīśvarī |
uṣṇīṣo vajrahaṃkāro raudrākṣī krodharājñī vajrapāṇirdhadhātuvatrā viṣṇuryamo jayo nāga ete ūdhyaṃ - vaktrāt śūnyāt sphāritā bahuguṇanilayā iti sarvakarmasiddhidā vajracihnā akṣobhya- mudritāḥ śyāmākāśavarṇata iti |
evamakṣobhyakuladevatāspharaṇādikaṃ suviśuddha- dharmadhātu jñānata iti |
idānīṃ vajrasatvakulaspharaṇamucyate - guhye śuddhādityādi |
iha guhyacakre śuddhajñānaskandhāt śuddhajñānadhātornīlatriśūkavajracihno vajrasattvo vijñānamudritaḥ |
evaṃ jñānadhātuḥ |
prajñā nīlā sumbharājo vajravego atinīlā krodheśvarī rudro jambhala: samantabhadraḥ śabdavajrā vijayanāga ete adhovaktrāt jñānadharmāt sphāritāḥ sakala- janahitāḥ prāṇināṃ mokṣahetoḥ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānato nīlavarṇā nīlākṣobhyamūrtyā vajrasattvena mudritāḥ sarvakarmasādhakā iti |
evaṃ ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaṭ kuladevatānāṃ sphāraṇaṃ jagadarthakaraṇaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣādṛdhvaṃ kālacakrasya janakatvena siddhamiti niyamaḥ |
atrākṣobhyo nīlenākṣobhyarūpeṇa mudritaḥ śyāmo vijñāna- skandhaḥ śyāmenākṣobhyeṇa nīlo jñāna skandhaḥ |
atha kāyavākcittajñāna catuḥ- kulabhedena yatra mudraṇam, tatra vairocanaḥ śuklaścandradharmataḥ, amitābho raktaḥ sūryadharmataḥ, akṣobhyaḥ kṛṣṇo rāhudharmataḥ vajrasattvaḥ pītaḥ kālāgnidharmata iti |
atha ādhārādheya- yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ sambandhena mudraṇaṃ sādhanāpaṭaloktavidhinā |
tathā hi-akṣobhyo vairocanasya mudraṇam, vairocano amitābhasya, amitābho ratnasaṃbhavasya, ratnasaṃbhavo'moghasiddheriti mudraṇaniyamaḥ |
evaṃ tredhātukamaheśvaro'nantacakrī sarvatantrāntare veditavyaḥ, trikule pañcakule ceti || 17||
idānīṃ prajñātantra spharaṇamucyate- dikpatre locanādyā vidiśidalagataṃ raktapūrṇa kapālaṃ pratyālīḍho'rkamūrdhni pralayaghananibhākrāntasomeśvaro vai |
mālābaddhaḥ kapālairjalanidhivadano herukaḥ sūryabāhu- mudrāhivyāghracarmā pralayagajapaterdāryacarmāsthidhārī || 18 ||
dikpatra ityādi |
iha kila cakrasaṃvare vajraḍākinyādayaścatasro locanādyā: pṛthivyādisvabhāvāḥ śāntikādisiddhidāḥ |
tāśca punaścittavaktrādikṛṣṇavarṇādibhinnāḥ satyo na pṛthivyādikarmasiddhidāḥ syuḥ |
tathāha - pītena stambhanaṃ kāryaṃ śvetena śāntikaṃ bhuvi |
vaśyākṛṣṭī ca raktena kṛṣṇenoccāṭanādikam || iti |
ataḥ kṛṣṇavarṇavaśād vajraḍākinī vāyudhātuḥ, raktavarṇaṃto lāmā tejodhātuḥ, pītavarṇataḥ khaṇḍarohā pṛthvīdhātuḥ śuklamukhavarṇataḥ rūpiṇī toyadhātuḥ |
evaṃ yathā cittavāgjñānakāyamukhānāṃ kṛṣṇaraktapītaśvetavarṇā yathākramam, tathā taddigvibhāge gatānāṃ pracaṇḍādīnāmapi nava nava devīnāṃ varṇo veditavyo mahālakṣābhighāne |
iti bhagavato niyamaḥ |
tenālpavīryavatāṃ mañjudhiyā'tra kālacakre laghūpadeśena sa eva prapañcaḥ prakāśita iti |
dikpatre locanādyā iti paścimadale locanā varṇataḥ, khaṇḍarohā pītā gudanāḍī-
svabhāvataḥ, pṛthvīvarṇato jñānavaktraṃ paścimaṃ pītaṃ kālacakrasya herukasya cihnādisvabhāvata iti
uttaradale toyavarṇato rūpiṇī śuklā uttaraṃ kāyavaktraṃ śuklaṃ lalanāsvabhāvataḥ
dakṣiṇe lāmā raktā tejovarṇato vāgvaktraṃ raktaṃ rasanāsvabhāvataḥ
pūrvadale vajraḍākinī kṛṣṇā vāyuvarṇataḥ cittavaktraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mūtranāḍīsvabhāvata iti
evamavadhūtīsvabhāvena vajravārāhī śyāmā śaṅkhinīsvabhāvataḥ
kālacakrī heruko bhagavān nīlavarṇa iti
evaṃ cittavākkāyacakrāreṣu pūrve'gnau kṛṣṇā ḍākinyaḥ, dakṣiṇe nairṛtye raktāḥ, paścime vāyavye pītāḥ, uttareśāne śuklā:
evamaṣṭaśmaśāneṣu devyo veditavyāḥ
āsāṃ vispharaṇena karmaprasārādikaṃ tantroktaṃ vajrapāṇikṛtaṭokayā baṭsāhasrikayā boddhavyaṃ laghutantre
tenātra na likhitam
viviśidalagatamityādi subodham
evaṃ samājo māyājāla- vimalaprabhāyāṃ mapi prasiddhaṃ subodham tenātra na likhitam
cittavāvakāyavajraspharaṇamātrata iti
atra mūlatantre sūtralakṣaṇam - dvāvekaraca trayaḥ sārdhā ardho hyeko yugaḥ śaśī
yuga eko yugaścaiko bhāgo dvātriṃśadādiṣu
iti
śeṣaṃ prākārādyaṃ kālacakramaṇḍalavaditi
18
devībhiḥ kṛṣṇaraktāmalaśaśitanubhistrīṇi cakrāṇi bāhye ṣaḍvajrāḥ krodhajābhidvibhujaśaśimukhābhistathā bhūtajābhiḥ
dvāre caṇḍāḥ śavasthā vidiśi ca niyatāḥ kartikā śuktihastā vīrāṇāṃ vedahaste kuliśaḍamaruko vajrakhaṭvāṅgaghaṇṭe
19
śrīmeroraṣṭadikṣu kṣitijalavalaye sarvapīṭhopapīṭhaṃ kṣetraṃ chandohamelāpakacitibhuvane vahnivāyvośca madhye
evaṃ deśe nagaryāṃ diśividiśigatā devatā veditavyāḥ prajñātantrābhidhānaṃ tribhuvanaguruṇā sphāritaṃ pūrvavaktrāt
20
garbhe cakraṃ navāṃśaṃ śaśiravikamale maṇḍalādardhabhāgaṃ vajrastambhāvalībhyāṃ racitamapi mahāskandhadhātvādhidevam
bāhye prākārabhittau viṣayaviṣayiṇaścandrasūryāsanasthāḥ sūryasthā dvārapālāstvaparagaṇakulaṃ vedikāyāṃ samantāt
21
ityādī ṣaṭ prakāraṃ gaditamapi tathā yogatantraṃ samājaṃ krodhāḥ koṇeṣu pārśve viṣayaviṣayiṇo jambhalo dhānyahastaḥ
vākpādau pāṇipāyū bhagaraviśaśinaḥ kālanāḍīsvabhāvā:
prajñopāyaṃ samastaṃ trimukharasabhujaṃ sphāritaṃ savyavaktrāt
22
cakraṃ garbhe trikoṇe bhavati narapate maṇḍalād vai tribhāgaṃ dhātuskandhādhidevaṃ punaraparapuṭe śabdavajrādayo'ṣṭau
bāhyasthe maṇḍale ve daśadiśi niyataṃ krodhavṛndaṃ ravisthaṃ tasmin prākārabhittau jaladhihatayugā bodhisattvāḥ samastāḥ
23
yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ māyājālaṃ tribhedaṃ trimukharasabhujaṃ devatākāyabhedāt kalpādyaṃ yatsamastaṃ jaḍahṛdayavaśāt sphāritaṃ vāmavaktrāt
tantraṃ yogānuviddhaṃ dviguṇamiha mahāsaṃvaraṃ ḍākinīnāṃ ṣaṭcakreḥ ṣaṭakule nṛpa citibhuvaneḥ sphāritaṃ paścimāsyāt
24
sūtreḥ ṣaḍrandhrabhāgeścalavalayagatairmaṇḍalaṃ sūtrayitvā garbhāt ṣaṭṣaḍvibhāgerapi kamaladalaṃ karṇikārdhena yuktam
kartī cakābja ratne ra sivarakuliśaiścāvalīṃ niryūhaṃ dvāramarkeṝtubhirapi dvidvibhāge- rasairvedikāhārabhūmim
25

aṣṭadvārāṇi kuryād diśividiśi mahāmaṇḍalaṃ vṛttameta- nmadhye'bjaṃ viśvavaṇaṃ raviśaśipuṭitaṃ cāsanaṃ karṇikāyām
26

pītā nīlā ca bhūmistvasitamapi bhavecchayāmavarṇaṃ ca cakraṃ ṣaḍbhāgairaṅgabhūmirjanakasukhavaśād dvāracakrāṇi tadvat
27
bāhye dvayaṣṭaśmaśānānyapi ca kulavaśād garbhadevyastathāṣṭo aṣṭau patre kapālānyamṛtarasayutānyaṣṭapatreṣu devyaḥ
bāhye lāsyādidevyo diśividiśi mahānāgarājāstathāṣṭo tadvāhye bhūmitoyānalacalavalayānyeva vajrāvalī ca
28
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- sūtrairityādi
iha paścimāsyāt jñānavaktrāt mahācakrasaṃvarastricakrasaṃvarasya dviguṇo yataḥ ṣaṭcakaiḥ ṣoḍaśaśmaśānairiti
tenāsya maṇḍalaṃ vṛttamaṣṭadvāra viśodhanāyeti, ato'sya maṇḍalasya lakṣa [ namucyate - sūtraiḥ ṣaḍrandhrabhāgeriti
ṣaṇṇavati- vibhāgeḥ, calavalayagatairiti lokadhātau caturlakṣayojana gavairvāyuvalayānteḥ
śarīre caturhastagatairiti
maṇḍalaṃ sūtrayitvā ṣaṇṇavatisūtresteṣu madhye ṣaṭṣa vibhāgaiḥ pratyekaṃ cakraṃ kuryādevaṃ cakraṃ ṣaṭṣavibhāgeriti
api kamalavalaṃ kaṇikārdhena yuktaṃ ṣaḍvibhāgairiti
evaṃ saptasu dvācatvāriṃśadvibhāgāḥ pūrve evaṃ paścime dakṣiṇe uttare'pi, sarveṣāmekatra caturaśītibhāgā gatāḥ
ṣaṇṇavatiṣu śeṣerdvādaśavibhāgeṣu ṣaḍvibhāgeḥ pūrvabhūmiḥ, paścimāpyevaṃ vāme dakṣiṇe'pyevaṃ sarvadikṣu sūtrāṇi śuddhānīti tatra pūrvokta- ṣaḍvibhāgamadhye catubhiḥ padmadalaṃ dvābhyāṃ kaṇikārdham
evaṃ cakrasyārāścatubhiḥ, dvābhyāṃ cihnāvalī kaṇikādīnāṃ madhye padmaṃ ṣoḍaśadalaṃ padmadalabāhye kaṇikāvalī nīlā
tasyā bhūmistale śyāmā
' evaṃ cakrārāśca nīlā iti jñānadhātucakre
tataḥ pṛthvī dhātucakre cakrāvalī pītā cakrārāstathā, kṛṣṇā tale bhūmiḥ
toyadhātucakre padmāvalī śuklā cakrārāśca bhūmistale raktā
tejodhātucakre ratnāvalī raktā cakārāśca, śvetā tale bhūmiḥ
vāyudhātucakre kṛṣṇā khaḍgāvalī cakārāśca pītā tale bhūmiḥ
ākāśadhātu- cakre śyāmā vajrāvalī cakrārāśca, tale bhūmirnīlā
tataḥ ṣaḍbhāgai raṅgabhūmirjanaka- mukhavaśāditi
pūrve kṛṣṇāmno ca dakṣiṇenairṛtye raktā, paścime vāyavye pītā, uttara īśāne śvetā, dvāracakrāṇi tadvadityaṣṭamahāśmaśānacakrāṇi
aṣṭadvārāvasāne - ṣṭaśmaśānacakrāṇyaṣṭra' dvārāntareṣu
evaṃ ṣoḍaśaśmaśānacakrāṇyaṣṭārāṇi, garbhapadmāda leṣvaṣṭī devyaḥ, aṣṭadaleṣu kapālānyamṛtapūrṇāni, aṣṭadevyantarāntareṣu ṣaṭcakrāṣṭāreṣu poṭhopapīṭhādi- devyo'ṣṭacatvāriṃśat
evaṃ vīrāśca veditavyāḥ
bāhye vedikāyāṃ nāgarājāno'ṣṭī, toraṇastambhamūle, lāsyādayo dvāratoraṇe, śeṣaṃ dvārādilakṣaṇaṃ kālacakramaṇḍalavad veditavyamiti
25-28
idānīṃ ṣaṭ cakārādiṣu devībījānyucyante- dīrghe hrasvaḥ svaraiścāpi sa ta pa ṭa ca ka kṣmādiśūnyādivarṇā vāyvādyāreṣu dīrghā ravicaraṇavaśāt kṣmādidhātusvabhāvāḥ
ūrdhvādyāreṣu hrasvāḥ śaśicaraṇavaśāt svasvacakāra mūrdhni dvāre dvārāntarāle gaganatalagatā hakṣayuktāḥ savargāḥ
29
dīrgherityādi
dīrgheḥ pañcasvaraḥ lū ū ṛ ī ā iti
hrasvaiśca bha i ṛ ula ityebhibhinnāḥ sa ta pa ṭa ca kānāṃ vargāṇāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ kṣmādiśūnyādivarṇā vāyvādhāreṣu dīrghā iti
iha jñānaca savargo'dhidaivaḥ, tasya slū vāyavyāre, pū īśāne, ṣa nairṛtye, śī āgneyyām, kā pātāle, vāyavyo 'taraśmaśānacakramadhye etāḥ pañca ḍākinyo vṛścika lagne pañcamaṇḍalanāyikāḥ sarvasattvānāṃ prāṇavāhata iti saṃhārakrameṇa dakṣiṇa- nāsāpuṭe
tato dhanurlagne ūrdhve - ka vāyavya paścimaśmaśānacakramadhye jñānacakrapūrvāre śi dakṣiṇeṣū, uttare pu, paścime sḷ iti vāmanāḍī pravāho dhanulagne
tataḥ pṛthvīcakre tavargo'dhidaivaḥ
tasya tla vāyavye, thū īśe, dṛ nairṛtyāre, dhī agnau nā pātāle vāyavya-
tla dvāre śmaśānacakramadhya iti kanyālagne

tatastoyadhātucakre pavargo'dhidevaḥ
tasya pla vāyavye, phū īśe, bṛ nairṛtye, bhī agnau mā pātāle īśadvāre
evaṃ karka lagne'dhi- devatāḥ
tathā siṃhalagne ākāśe ma, uttaradvāre pūrvāre bhi, dakṣiṇe bṛ, uttare phu, paścime plu iti
tataśtejaścakre ṭavargo'dhidevaḥ
tasya ṭla vāyavyāre, īśe ṭhū, ḍṛ nairṛtye, agnau ḍho, adho ṇā nairṛtyadvāra iti vṛṣalagne
tato mithune ṇa ūrdhve, dakṣiṇadvāraśmaśāne pūrvāre ḍhi, dakṣiṇe ḍū, uttare ṭhu, paścime dala iti
tato vāyucakre cavargo'dhidaivaḥ
tasya cala vāyavyāre, 'īśāre chū, nairṛtye ju, agnī jhī, pātāle trā agnidvāre mīnalagne iti
tato meṣalagne ūrdhvaṃ tra, pūrvadvāraśmaśānacakre pūrvāre jhi, dakṣiṇe jū, uttare chu, paścime cūla iti
tata ākāśadhātucakre kavargo'dhidevaḥ
tasya vāyavyāre klū, īśe khū, nairṛtyegu, agnau ghī, adho ṅā āgneya dakṣiṇadvāramadhye śmaśānacakropari iti makaralagne
tataḥ kumbhalagne ūrdhve ṅa, āgneya' pūrvadvāramadhye pūrvāre ghi, dakṣiṇāre uttarāre paścimāre kla iti
dvāre dvārāntarāle talagaganagatā hakṣayuktāḥ sabargā iti
iha ṣaḍvargāṇāṃ śūnyamaṇḍalavāhakā dvādaśavarṇā dīrghahrasva kā ka nā na mā ma ṇā ṇa ñāña ṅā ṅa iti dvādaśaśmaśānadevīnām
anye catuḥ sandhyābhedena tritrilagnānte catvāro varṇā ha hā kṣa kṣā iti caturṣu vāmeśānadakṣiṇanairṛtyaśmaśāneṣu
evaṃ ṣoḍaśaśmaśāneṣu ṣoḍaśa devyaḥ, ṣaṭcakreṣvaṣṭacatvāriṃśadgarbhapadmadaleṣvaṣṭadevyaḥ
āsāṃ bījāni a pūrvadale, aḥ dakṣiṇadale, aṃ uttaradale, ā paścimadale, ha agnidale, haḥ nairṛtyadale, haṃ īśānadale, hā vāyavyadala iti tāsāmantarāntareṣvaṣṭadaleṣvaṣṭa- kapālāni
madhye vajraḍākinī kālacako heruko bhagavān nāyako hū~ pha~ āsāṃ ḍākinīnāṃ ḍākānāṃ ca bojānte vajram, tena saha nāma slavajrā pūvatrā ityādi klu'vajrāparyantam
āsāṃ ḍākā bhāvabhedenāṣṭacatvāriṃśat sivajñaḥ ṣṛvajraḥ
gṛ, khu, kāna nā ma mā ṇa ṇā ña ñā ṅa ṅā

stṛvajrā' sivatra iti prajñopāyaḥ
pūvajrā bṛvatra iti
evaṃ sarvatra vakṣapramāṇamiti
evaṃ maṇḍale yajeta dviguṇaṃ ḍākinījālasaṃvaramiti niyamaḥ
iha vṛścikadhanurādinā yo vilomena nyāsaḥ, sa prapañcenoktaḥ
atra dvādaśāṅganirodhena vivṛtyā paramārthasatyato dhanurvṛści- kādivilomena pīṭhopapīṭhādinyāsaḥ
saṃvṛtyā makarakumbhādikrameṇopapīlavā di- nyāso dvādaśāṅgapratītyataḥ
yathā tricakrasaṃvare eka: pracaṇḍā caṇḍākṣyādinā vilomena pīṭhādinyāsaḥ, dvitīyāṃ mahāvīryādinā
anukrameṇopaśmaśānādinā
atra loka- vyavahāre yā saṃvṛtiḥ sā vivṛtiriti jñeyā
evaṃ vāyvādhāreṣu dīrghā ravicaraṇavaśād dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ prāṇacaraṇavaśāditi
ūrdhvācāreṣu hasvā: śaśicaraṇa- vaśāditi vāmanāḍyāṃ prāṇa' saṃcāravaśāditi nyāsa uktaḥ
krameṇa punaḥ saṃvṛtyā upapīlavādinā makarādimaṇḍaleṣu ka ca ṭa pa ta sa vargāṇāṃ varṇā dīrghā hasvā deyāḥ
kakārādayo lakārādisvarabhinnā vivṛtyā kādayo hrasvā akārādisvarabhinnā dhanurvṛścikādibhedeneti
evaṃ pṛthivyādayo vāyvādyāreṣu dakṣiṇamaṇḍale vāhata ūrdhvādyāreṣu ākāśādayo vāmamaṇḍalapravāhata ityadhyātmani niyamaḥ
ḍākinyo hasvabhāvāḥ śaśadharavadanāḥ kartikā śuktihastā ālīḍhāḥ svasvapātrairdanu jarudhirapā muktakeśā vivastrāḥ
pāde kaṭyāṃ lalāṭe śravaṇagalakare ghurghurādyasthimudrā mālā khaṇḍaiḥ kapālaiḥ śirasi kaṭitale pañcavarṇaijinānām
dvayāsyā ḍākāḥ kareṣu supaviḍamaruko vajrakhaṭvāṅgaghaṇṭe kaṇṭhe śrīmuṇḍamālā śirasi ca mukuṭo vajrapaṭṭe maṇiśca
śrīkaṇṭhī nūpurogho rucakamapi kare mekhalā kuṇḍalāni mālā 'pūrṇe : kapālaiḥ sakalatanugatā daṃṣṭriṇo bhasmamudrā
bharturmālā kapālaiḥ śirasi ca galake muṇḍamālā śatāsyai- rardhendurva maulau svakaṭikaragataṃ dvīpicarmebhacarma
māreśī pādamūle śaśiravihutabhuṅa maṇḍale'ṅge ca bhasma mudrā nāgendrayuktā pralayaśikhinibhā ḍākinī cumbamānā
dikpatre ḍākinīnāmadhikaḍamaruko vajrakhaṭvāṅgameva viṇmūtrāsṛṅnumāṃsavidiśi dalagataṃ śukrapātraṃ kare ca
yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ tadvaccāṣṭau ca devyo'pyubhayasukhasamāpatticakraṃ samantāt pītā śvetā'ruṇābhā'sitaharitavapurnīlavarṇāḥ svadikṣu
varṇāścihnāni bhartujinajanakakule pūrvavat tryakṣijāla- manyonyāliṅganaṃ ca svaparakulavaśād veditavyaṃ samantāt
nānādye: skandhabhūtaviṣayaviṣayibhiścānyapañcendriyādye- dikkṣmābhidigbale: śrījina sutavasitābhiśca devyo viśodhyāḥ

idānīṃ devīnāṃ pīṭhādisthānamucyate- merodikṣvaṣṭabhedediśividiśigataṃ sarvapīṭhopapīṭhaṃ kṣetra chandohamelāpakacitibhuvanaṃ veśmavāyvadhaṃsīmnaḥ

merorityādi
iha meroraṣṭadikṣu ūrdhve yoginīcakraṃ vāyuvalayāntam, adhobhūmyāṃ sattvārthāya sañcāraḥ
evaṃ samudravalaye'gnivalaye vāyuvalaye ākāśānta iti pañcalakṣayo- janaṃ yāvallokadhātumaṇḍale sattvānāṃ śarīre keśāntaṃ vyāpakatvam, bhagavato yoginī saṃcārata iti
iha merumūrdhni pañcāśatsahasrayojanaṃ garbhapadmaṃ ṣoḍaśa 'dalaṃ bhagavataḥ
tasya tribhāgā kaṇikā, kaṇikopari sūryamaṇḍalam, sūryopari kālacakrasya dakṣiṇapādatale māraḥ, vāmapādatale kleśaḥ, sa ca rudra iti
aṣṭadaleṣu dhūmādyā vajraḍākinyo'ṣṭau
tāsāmantarāntare daleṣvaṣṭau kapālānyamṛtapūrṇāni
evaṃ ṣoḍaśadalamānandacakraṃ ṣoḍaśānandabhedena devyāḥ kapālāni ca
bhagavāṃścatubindudharatvāccaturmukhaḥ, caturviṃśatipakṣarodhata: caturviṃśatikaraḥ, adhikamāsa- pakṣayonirodhato dvayadhikajinakaraḥ, pūrvokto vajravega iti
bhagavatyā ekamukhaṃ śūnyatā, bhujadvayamanimittāpraṇihite, kartikākapālamudrādayo'nabhisaṃskāra iti
tataḥ padmabāhye meroradikṣu digvibhāgena pañcaviṃśatsahasrayojanaṃ bhūmivalayamānena jñānadhātucakram, tasya tribhāgabhūmyāṃ kartikāvalī, bhāgadvayenāṣṭāram
evaṃ samudrārdhe pṛthvīcakram, aparārdhe toya- cakram
evamagnivalayārdhe vahnicakram, aparārdhe vāyucakram
evaṃ vāyuvalayārdhe ākāśacakram, aparārdhe rajobhūmiḥ
ākāśavalaye ṣoḍaśa śmaśānānīti niyamaḥ
iha hi keśāntaṃ pañcahastaṃ śarīram tenākāśamaṇḍale bāhyaśmaśānāni, tadviśuddhayā lokadhātuśca pañca- lakṣayojanāni mahācakrasaṃvare
evaṃ yathākrameṇa meroraṣṭadikṣu jñānadhātucakre poṭhāni vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- digāreṣu dhanunirodhena jarāmaraṇanirodhata upapīṭhāni vidigāreṣu vṛścikanirodhena jātinirodhataḥ
evaṃ śmaśānadvayam
tataḥ pṛthvīdhātucakre digāreṣu catvāri kṣetrāṇi tulānirodhena bhavanirodhataḥ

oṃ trailokyavijaya 'haḥ vacce huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti mīne
oṃ mahāvīreśvara “aḥ vace huṃ hūṃ phaṭ meṣe
oṃ vajrabhairava haḥ vacce huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti vṛṣe
oṃ vajrakāya a: vatre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti mithune
oṃ vajragātra haṃ vace huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti kakaṭe
oṃ vajranetra aṃ vajje huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti siṃhe
oṃ vajra śrotra hā vatre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti kanyā saṃkrāntī
oṃ vajraghrāṇa 'ha vatre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti tulāyām
oṃ vajrajihva kṣā vace huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti vṛścike
oṃ vajradanta kṣa vatre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti dhanuḥsaṃkrāntī śvāsena sārdhaṃ japet
iti dvādaśasaṃkrāntī jñānamaṇḍalajāpaḥ
oṃ vajranakha klū vace ghi vajra huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti makarapṛthvīmaṇḍale sapādekādaśonaṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatajāpaḥ
evaṃ sarvatra ṣaṣṭimaṇḍaleṣu
oṃ vajrakeśa khū vacce gṛ vajra huṃ huṃ phaḍityudake
oṃ vajraloma gṛ vastre khu vastra huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti tejasi
oṃ vajrābharaṇa ghī vajre kla vajra huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti vāyau
oṃ vajrahāsa 'dā vatre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti ākāśamaṇḍale śmaśāne bhagavānupāyastena dvitīyabījābhāvaḥ
evaṃ sarvatrākāśamaṇḍale jñeyaḥ
oṃ vajragīta 'da vastre huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti kumbhākāśamaṇḍale śvāsajāpaḥ
oṃ vajranṛtya ghi vajre kla vajra huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti vāyo
vajrāyudhaka gṛ vajre khū vajra huṃ hūṃ phaḍityagnau
oṃ vajrakodhādhipate khu vatre gṛ vastra huṃ hūṃ phaḍityudake
oṃ vajraḍāka kḷ vastre gho vajra huṃ huṃ phaḍiti pṛthvīmaṇḍale śvāsa jāpaḥ
evaṃ kavargo gataḥ

tataḥ karkaṭalagne oṃ arūpadhātugatānākṛṣyākṛṣya plū vastre bhi vastra huṃ hūṃ phaḍiti pṛthvīmaṇḍale śvāsajāpaḥ

evaṃ mālāmantraṃ pratidinaṃ 'śvāsacakrasaṃkhyaṃ japet idānīmavadhūtīśvāsaiḥ kāyavākcittajñānapañcacakaiḥ sarvabuddhasaṃvaramucyate-

ye śvāsā ityādi

atra kapālāni na bhavanti vīrāśca aṣṭau rūpaviśuddhayā pṛthvīcakre, aṣṭau saṃjñāśuddhayā toyacake, aṣṭau vedanāśuddhayā tejaścakre, aṣṭau saṃskāra- viśuddhayā vāyucake, aṣṭo vijñānaviśuddhayā''kāśacakre, aṣṭau 'jñānaśuddhayā'ṣṭaśmaśā- neṣu

idānīṃ bāhye vā'dhyātmani devatāsanaśuddhirucyate- pañcatrayacandusūryā vārā: saptārkapūrṇā viṣayaguṇavaśādāsanānyabdhicārā dinakaraśaśinaścāparāṇyāsanāni
kṛṣṇā śvetārkapūrṇā dinakaraśaśinaścāparāṇyāsanāni dvārā: saptārkapūrṇāḥ samaviṣamapade sūryacandrāsanāni || 40 ||
pañcetyādi
ihaikavīrasyāsanaṃ candraḥ śukaṃ sūryo raja iti pañcadhātūnāṃ prajñādharma-
svāt candraguṇāḥ pañca viṣayāste'tra locanādīnāṃ candrāsanānyākāśadhātuparyantam
guṇatrayavaśād ratnasaṃbhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhīnāṃ śroṇi sūryāsanāni
caturbhūtātmakaṃ mahā- rūpamiti, caturdhāturdhāmatvād vairocanasya candrāsanam, jñānāvidhamitvāt sūryāsanaṃ veda- nādīnām arūpitvaṃ tu śeṣāṇām' iti vacanāt
evaṃ pañca candramaṇḍalāni catvāri sūryamaṇḍalānīti sattvarajastamaḥ samatābhedena
evaṃ navātmaka: samājaḥ skandhadhātūnāṃ guṇaviṣayādhārataḥ, yathā garbhe jātakavijñānādīnāṃ tathā devatāyāmapi punaraparāṇi catvāri krodhāsanāni
abdhicārā iti sūryasya ravikādhana ṛṇavṛddhihānivaśāt krodhānāṃ sūryāsanāni
evaṃ krodhānāṃ cārā iti trayodaśātmaka: samājaḥ, evaṃ saptavārā: sūryā- sanāni, dvādaśapūrṇāścandramaṇḍalāni, ekonaviṃśadātmaka: samājastṛtīyaḥ, kṛṣṇapūrṇā amāvāsyā prativarṣe dvādaśa, śvetapūrṇā paurṇamāsyo dvādaśa, evaṃ caturviṃśatiḥ sūryacandrā- sanāni nāyakasya kulavaśāccandro vā sūryo vā
evaṃ pañcaviṃśadātmakaḥ samājo devatā- nāmādhārabhedata iti
punasteṣu madhye'parasaptavārāsanāni kṣiptāni dvātriṃśadāsanāni bhavanti
pañcaskandhacaturdhātūnāṃ dvādaśāyatanānāmekādaśakrodhānāmiti dvātriṃśadātmaka: samājaḥ
punaḥ samaviṣamapade prāṇasya gatiḥ sūryāsanamāgatiścandrāsanam, tena catustri- śadātmakaḥ samājaḥ
evaṃ ṣaṭkāro yathā garbhe bālasya dhātūnāmādhārādheyamelāpakastathā devatānāṃ mīlanamitinyāyāt ṣaṭprakāraḥ samājaḥ || 40
idānīṃ tricakrasaṃvare āsanaśuddhirucyate- ārāścandrārkacārā: - kamaladalacaturdvārakoṇāsanāni bhūyaścārā dviguṇyā dinakaraśaśinaścāsanānyeva tāni
pūrṇā vārākacārāḥ samaviṣamapade cāsanānyabdhicārāḥ sūrye vā pūrṇacandre bhavati kulavaśānnāyakaḥ saṃpuṭe vā || 41
ārā ityādi
iha tricakre ārāścatuviśatiḥ, te ca bhūtā bhūteṣuvedā guṇakara- śaśina iti pūrvoktāścandracārāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ teṣāṃ caturviṃśati- stricakārā bhavanti
ekaḥ padmavaladevatāyāḥ pañcaviṃśatitamaḥ pretāsanamiti
arkacārā rasayugaśaśina ityekādaśa, teṣāṃ garbhapadmadaleṣu trayaḥ, śeṣā aṣṭau aṣṭaśmaśāneṣu śavarūpeṇā- vasthitāḥ, ārā ityādhāraḥ
śavā iti śavāni devīvāhanāni
evaṃ tricakrasaṃvaraḥ
bhūyaḥ pūrvāparabhedena cārā dviguṇā mahāsaṃvare devīnāmāsanāni dvāsaptatirbhavanti
evaṃ ṣaṭcakrasaṃvaraḥ
māyājāle pūrṇāścaturviṃśatiḥ śuklakṛṣṇāścandrasūryāsanāni, tathā sapta- 'vārā: saptasūryāsanāni, arkacārā ekādaśa sūryāsanāni

idānīṃ vāyvādidevatānāṃ vaktraśuddhirucyate-- vāyoḥ sparśāsyamekaṃ tvaparaguṇavaśānmudraṇāsyaṃ dvitīyaṃ rūpasparśadvayāsyaṃ bhavati ca śikhino mudraṇāsyaṃ tṛtīyam
sparśo rūpaṃ rasāsyaṃ mukhamapi payaso mudraṇāsyaṃ catuthaṃ gandhādyaṃ sparśajāntaṃ guṇamukhamavaneḥ pañcamaṃ mudraṇāsyam ||42 ||
[ jñāna- vāyorityādi

evaṃ vāyoḥ sparśāsyamekaṃ tvaparaguṇavaśānmudraṇāsyaṃ dvitīyaṃ śirasi, na mukhasthāne
evaṃ 'rūpasparśa- viṣayadharmeṇa mukhadvayaṃ tejaso mudraṇāsyaṃ tṛtīyam
guṇena tena vedanayā'pi vijñānamudraṇam
tathā sparśarūparasāsthaṃ mukhatrayaṃ payasa ākāśalakṣaṇaḥ śabdo mudraṇāsyaṃ caturtham, tena saṃjñayā vijñānamudraṇam

tena rūpaskandhasyāpi vijñānaskandho mudraṇamiti siddham || 42

ekaṃ miśraṃ catuṣke samasukhaphaladaṃ pañcakaṃ dvitrimi prajñopāyaṃ daśāsyaṃ trividhaguṇavaśād dvādaśāsyaṃ caturdhā ||43||
śūnyaṃ pañcaprakāraṃ pañcākṣaro mahāśūnyaḥ iti vacanād mukhapañcakamākāśadhātorvijñānaskandhasya mudraṇāsyaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ jñānaskandham

iha yasyā devatāyāḥ svacihnaṃ dakṣiṇe prathamakaratale gacchati, tasya sthāne vajracihnaṃ yāti
evaṃ samāja- niyamaḥ
yathā samāje yo buddho madhye viśati tasya sthāne'kṣobhyo gacchati, tathā cihne'pi niyamaḥ
evaṃ pañcamukhaṣaṇmukhayorekādaśāsyaṃ vijñānajñānadhātvoḥ pañcānanaṣaṇmukhayoḥ pañcākṣara mahāśūnya binduśūnyaṣaḍakṣarayoḥ
punastayoḥ parasparāpekṣa- katvena dvātriṃśadāsyaḥ samāja iti niyamaḥ || 43
idānīṃ kālabhedena vaktrādiśuddhirucyate- sandhyābhedāccaturdhā trividhamapi mukhaṃ lagnabhedāt trivarṇaṃ lagnārghād bāhubhedo viṣamamapi samāliṅganaṃ kāyacitte
sandhyāṅge pūrvapṛṣṭhe trimukharasabhujāliṅganaṃ madhyamāṅge prajñāsaṅgaḥ samāpattirapi hi hasitādyāni yāmābhisandhau ||44||
sandhyetyādi
iha dinastu bhagavān vajjī naktaṃ prajñā prakīrtitā iti niyamāt 'prahara praharasandhyābhedena bhagavāṃścaturmukho'ṣṭabhujo'rdha prahabhedenārdharātrānmadhyāhnaṃ yāvat, evaṃ prajñā'pi madhyāhlādardharātraṃ yāvaccaturmukhā aṣṭabhujeti
samāliṅganaṃ madhyāhne divārātryoḥ, viṣamamapi pūrvasandhyā - aparasandhyayodivārātryorhīnādhikavaśataḥ, tatra kutra- cid bhagavān pañcamukho daśabhujo bhagavatī trimukhā ṣaḍbhujā divāvṛddhitaḥ
niśāvṛddhito bhagavatī pañcamukhā bhagavāṃstrimukha ityādisiddhaḥ, ityekā kālacakraviśuddhiḥ
tathā dvitīyo- cyate - trividhetyādi
iha dvādaśarāśayo makarādayaḥ teṣāṃ madhye makarakumbhamīna- rāśayaścittavapacasya tamorajaḥsattvabhedena kṛṣṇaraktasitamukhāni lagnabhedāt trivarṇānīti
evaṃ meṣavṛṣamithuna mukhāni rajaḥ sattvatamobhedena raktasitakṛṣṇavarṇāni vāgvajrasyeti
karka siṃha kanyā mukhāni sattvatamorajobhedena śuklakṛṣṇa raktavadanāni kāyavajjrasyeti
tulāvṛścikadhanurlagnamukhāni tamorajaḥ sattvabhedena potaraktasitavarṇāni jñānavajrasyeti
lagnārthādvāhubhedo bhavati trivarṇaḥ
ṣaṇṇāṃ dvayordvayoryathāsaṃkhyaṃ mūlavaktrādinā vāmadakṣiṇayoḥ
evaṃ dvādaśāsyaścaturviṃśatikaro bhagavānekaḥ kālacakraḥ
atra pūrvoktānāṃ devatānāmeka- mukhacaturmukhādonāṃ yadviṣamasamāliṅganam, tatsandhyāṅge pūrva pṛṣṭhe pūrva sandhyāyā aparasandhyāyā melāpakaḥ, trimukhaṣaḍbhujānāṃ yatparasparamāliṅganaṃ tanmadhyamāṅge madhyāhnasandhyāyā ardharātrasandhyāyāḥ
evaṃ kāyacitte iti pañcamyarthe saptamī kāya bhāvabhedāditi
evaṃ pūrvāparasandhyā yoginītantrāliṅganaṃ viṣamasamāpattita ekamukhacaturmukhayoreka mukhādyaṣṭa- mukhādyorveti yogatantre samamukhabhujayorāliṅganaṃ tulyadivāniśākāle iti niyamaḥ kālaviśeṣeṇa
evam - bho. 'prahara' nāsti ca tamo'ntarajaḥ ca
'ardharātrasandhyāyāḥ nāsti vimalaprabhāyāṃ śubhāśubhajñaḥ kālajñaḥ samayajñaḥ samayī vibhuḥ
sattvendriyajño velajño vimuktitrayakovidaḥ || iti nāmasaṅgītvāṃ bhagavato niyamaḥ
[ jñāna- idānīṃ kriyātantrāṇyucyante - prajñetyādi
iha prajñāsaṅgo yoginītantraṃ, samāpattirapi yogatatre, pūrvāparasandhayogo madhyāhnārdharātrasaṃyoga iti siddhastantradvaye, hasitādyāni yāmābhisandhāviti
iha prathamapraharābhisandho hasitatantram, tṛtīyapraharā- bhisandho īkṣaṇatantram, pañcamapraharasandhau stanasparśatantram saptamapraharasandhau pāṇigrahaṇa - tantram, evaṃ caturdhā tantram
tatkasya hetoḥ ?
iha vāyukṛtsnānāṃ prajñāhasitamātreṇa spandasukhaṃ bhavati
tejaḥ kṛtsnānāmokṣaṇena sukhaṃ bhavati
udakakṛtsnānāṃ stanasparśana [ sukhaṃ bhavati
] pṛthvīkṛtsnānāṃ pāṇigrahaṇena sukhaṃ bhavati
evaṃ ṣoḍaśa rūpāvacarāḥ
akaniṣṭhādinā brahmakāyikaparyantameṣāṃ vistāro vakṣyamāṇe paramākṣarajñānasiddhī vaktavya iti
kāmāvacarāṇāṃ samāpattirdvandvayoga iti siddhāntaḥ
sarvatrārūpe vīta- rāgāṇāṃ sthitiḥ || 44 ||
idānīṃ kāyabhāvapraveśa ucyate- prajñāṅge raktapīte praviśati niyataḥ kālacakrasya bhāvaḥ prajñābhāvaḥ sitāṅge kaṣaṇaghananibhe vajriṇo vāmapūrvam
prajñābhāvena bhinnaṃ bhavati varatano vāmapūrvāṅgasaumyaṃ raudraṃ savyāparāṅgaṃ paramajinapaterbhāvabhinnaṃ tathaiva || 45 ||
prajñāṅga ityādi
iha prajñāṅge rakte tejodhātau pīte pṛthvīdhātau praviśati niyataḥ kālacakrasya bhāvaḥ prajñopāyo grāhyaḥ, yataḥ samāpattī yā prajñā sā upāya bhāvagrāhikā, yaścopāyaḥ sa prajñābhāvagrāhakaḥ
tena bhāvagrahaṇena sukhotpattiḥ tena kālacakrasya śvetaṃ toyadhātū raktāṅge'gnyaṅge praviśati samaraso bhavati
evaṃ kṛṣṇaḥ pītāṅge pṛthivyāṃ sama- raso vāyurbhavati
evaṃ dakṣiṇe paścime devatānāmiti
evaṃ prajñābhāvaḥ sitāṅga toya- dhātau raktabhāvaḥ praviśati
vajriṇo vāmāṅge devatānāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇavāyudhātau pṛthvībhāvaḥ pītaḥ pūrve viśati
evamūrdhvādho haritanīlayoḥ parasparaṃ saṃyogaḥ
evaṃ prajñābhāvena bhinaṃ bhavati varatanau sarvasattvānāṃ vāmapūrvaṃ ca somyam, raudraṃ savyāparāṅgaṃ paramajinapaterbhāva- bhinaṃ tathaiveti, ūdhvaṃ saumyamadho raudraṃ siddham || 45 ||
prajñābhinnaṃ jinasya pravaragaṇakulaṃ dvisvabhāvatvameti prajñāyā bhartṛbhinnaṃ vidiśi ca niyataṃ yoginīvṛndameva
paṭale, ślo. ] yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ yugmaṃ savyāvasavyaṃ raviśa śivapuṣāpyeva pūrvāparaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ pītaṃ ca nīlaṃ haritamapi tathākāśapātālasaṃstham ||46 ||
evaṃ 'prajñābhinaṃ jinasya pravaragaṇakulaṃ 'śukradhātubhūtaṃ toyavāyvākāśalakṣaṇaṃ kāyabhedena yattadeva bhāvabhedena rajodhātunirbhinnaṃ pṛthvītejojñānasvabhāvaṃ bhavatīti dvi- svabhāvaṃ pravaragaṇakulamiti devatāvṛndaṃ raktaṃ śuklaṃ ca bhavati, vāmadakṣiṇaṃ pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca bhavati, pūrvāparamūrdhvādho nīlaṃ śyāmaṃ ca bhavati
evaṃ prajñāyā gaṇakulaṃ bhartṛbhāva bhinaṃ vidiśi ca niyataṃ dvisvabhāvatvameti
nairṛtyeśāne śvetaṃ raktaṃ ca bhavati, vāyavye agno kṛṣṇaṃ pītaṃ ca bhavati dvisvabhāvamiti niyamaḥ
evamupāyavṛndaṃ diśigataṃ vidiśigataṃ yoginīvṛnyaṃ prajñāvṛndaṃ locanādikaṃ tārādikaṃ ceti
evaṃ yugmaṃ savyāvasa vyaṃ raviśa śivapuṣāpyeva pūrvāparaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ potaṃ ca nīlaṃ haritamapi tathā''kāśapātālasaṃsthaṃ devatāgaṇamiti niyamaḥ || 46 ||
prajñā raktāḥ sitānāṃ śaśadharadhavalā lohitānāṃ tathaiva pītānāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇā varakanakanibhāścāsitānāṃ kulānām
nīlānāṃ viśvavarṇāḥ punarapi haritānāṃ ca nīlāstathoktā evaṃ vai devatīnāṃ svakuladiśigatā devatā veditavyāḥ ||47 ||
ataḥ parakulāliṅganataḥ prajñā raktāḥ sitānāmupāyānāṃ bhavati śaśadhara- dhava lāḥ prajñā lohitānāmupāyānām
tathaiva pītānāmupāyānāṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ prajñā varakanakanibhāḥ potā: prajñāścāsitānāmiti kṛṣṇānāmupāyānāṃ kulānāṃ nīlānāṃ viśvavarṇā iti haritā: prajñāḥ
punarapi haritānāmupāyānāṃ nīlāḥ prajñāstathoktā iti nyāyaḥ
parakulāliṅganataḥ kriyāniṣpattiḥ svakulāliṅganena kriyāsiddhirnāsti, svātmani kriyāvirodhāt
na hi bhagajātīyānāṃ dhātūnāṃ bhagacintayā sukhotpādaḥ, evaṃ puruṣa- liṅgajātīnāṃ vā liṅgacintayā sukhotpādaḥ
ataḥ kālacakre svābhāḥ prajñā na bhavanti, svātmani kriyāvirodhāditi
athādarśapratibimbadharmeṇa svābhagrahaṇaṃ cittasya tadeva vicāryate
ihādarśe puruṣa pratibimbaṃ na stanakeśavatīsadṛśam, nāpi strībimbaṃ stanayoni- rahitam, tena svābhatā nāsti
tathā aparo'pi virodhaḥ
iha yadvimbasya vāmabhujeṣu cihnāni tānyādarśapratibimbasya dakṣiṇabhujeṣu gatāni
evaṃ yad vāmamukhaṃ taddakṣiṇam, (uttara)
bhoṭānusārī pāṭho'vagantavya:
bho Re Sig ( kecit ) ityadhikam
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- tadvarṇatāpi svābheti na bhavati, tena maṇḍalacakrabhāvanā parakulāliṅganena 'kālacakra- nyāyataḥ| aparatantrāntare 'punaḥ - yena yena hi bhāvena manaḥ saṃyujyate nṛṇām
tena tanmayatāṃ yāti viśvarūpo maṇiryathā || ( yo saṃ tenāstavyastakaraṃ (rā) vāco na vicāra kṣamā iti
paramārthataḥ punaḥ śūnyatābimba- bhāvanāyāṃ rūpabimbakalpanā nāsti, tena svābhāsvābha iti nāsti
sarvākārataḥ sarvavarṇato jñānajñeyayoḥ paramāṇudravyavikalparahitayoḥ svabhāvo nāsti
ato niḥsvabhāva- bhavabhāvanā ghaṭapaṭaśakaṭasenādikānāṃ kāryā, na prādeśikī sakalalokavyavahārarahiteti siddhānto mādhyamiko mantranaye tattvapaṭale nyāyata iti || 47 ||
prajñātantraṃ hi pūrvāt punaraparamukhādeva yogānuviddhaṃ savyāsyād yogatantraṃ gadati jinapatirvāmavaktrāt kriyādyam
yogācāraṃ hi pūrvāt punaraparamukhānmadhyagaṃ vai samastaṃ sūtrāntaṃ savyavaktrād gadati sitamukhācchuddhavaibhāṣikaṃ ca ||48||
ṛgvedaṃ paścimāsyādapi gadati yajurvāmavaktrājjinendraḥ savyāsyāt sāmavedaṃ paramaharikule'tharvaṇaṃ pūrvavaktrāt
pūrvāsyāt kolatantraṃ punaraparamukhād gāruḍaṃ bhūtatantraṃ siddhāntaṃ vāmavaktrādudayaravinibhād viṣṇudhamaṃ ca savyāt || 49 ||
pṛṣṭhāt sadyo nivṛttiḥ paramasitamukhād vāmadevaḥ pratiṣṭhā savyād vidyā tvaghoraḥ punaranilamukhānmārutabrahmaśāntyo (ntī ? )
śūnyāsyāt śūnya - īśau tribhuvanapatinā sphāritā laukikārthaṃ kṛt tretā dvāparaṃ vai kaliyugamaparaṃ pṛṣṭhavaktrādibhedāt ||50||
yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ pūrvāt sarvāstivādaṃ gadati jinapatiḥ sāmmitīyaṃ ca savyāt pṛṣṭhāsyāt sthāvarīyaṃ punarapi ca mahāsāṃghikaṃ vāmavaktrāt
mānuṣyaṃ pūrvavaktrāt punaraparamukhāt sphāritāstiyaṃgā ve savyāsyād bhūtadevāḥ paramasitamukhānnāra kāścāsurāśca ||52||
hṛccakrāt pūrvavaktrāt spharaṇamapi bhavennābhicakrāt parāsyāt saṃbhogāt savyavaktrād bhavati ca sahajād vāmavaktrāt samantāt |
cakrāṇāṃ vaktramadhyāt spharaṇanidhanatā yāti bhāvaḥ śarīre trailokyaṃ cāpi kṛtsnaṃ khalu jinajanakasyāsyabhedaiśca samyak || 53||
śrīcandrāccandrakānte sravati hi salilaṃ nirvikalpasvabhāvāt tadvat sarvajñadharmaḥ sravati jinapateḥ skandhamāśritya loke |
dharmastoyaṃ yathaiva vrajati samarasaṃ bījadhātvāśrayeṇa sattvānāṃ cittaśuddhayā bhavati bahuvidhaṃ pūrvakamaṃprabhāvāt || 54 ||
cintā sarvārthakartustribhuvananilaye nāsti cintāmaṇeśca cittasthaṃ tanna paśyantyaśubhaphalavaśāt karmaṇaḥ pāpasattvāḥ |
doṣaścintāmaṇerna hyaśubhaśubhaphalaṃ sarvasattvaḥ prabhuṅkte śuddhe sattve jinendraḥ sa ca bhavati naraḥ kiṃ jinenāpareṇa ||55||
jāto yenāṅkaro'sau vrajati sa janako jātabhāvād vināśaṃ jāto naṣṭasya tasya prabhavati janako bījarājasya bhūyaḥ |
yaḥ pūrvaḥ so'nyabhāvānmṛta iti vacanaṃ na svarūpeṇa jātaḥ śūnyād yenāgataḥ kṣmāṃ vrajati daśabalastena saukhyakṣaṇena || 56 ||
sokhyāt saukhyānuraktaḥ kṣaṇa iha sahajo nānyabhāvānuraktaḥ skandhā ye tena jātāḥ punarapi janakāste'sya sokhyakṣaṇasya |
śuddhāddharmastato'nyaḥ punarapi ca tato'nyaśca tasmādviśuddho bījānmūlāni śākhā : kusumaphalamivāropitācchuddhabhūmyām ||57||
bhūvāryagniśca vāyū rasaparamaraso cāṇavaḥ ṣaṭ prakārā gandhādyekekahīnā viṣayavirahitāścāntimā jñānadṛśyāḥ |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ kāmā rūpāstvarūpā yamayamaśaśinaścāntimo dharmadhātuḥ sarvākārāḥ sadā te'cyutasukhasahajādhārabhūtāḥ samantāt ||58||
anyonyaṃ skandhabhūtā viṣaya viṣayiṇo'nyonyamārāstva vidyāḥ pañcānyonyānuraktā rasagatiṣu gatā duḥkhasokhyakṣaṇasya |
sarvākārāḥ samantānna paraguṇaratāḥ saṃsthitā'bhedavajrā anyonyaṃ kṣmādighāto punarapi ca gatā mukhyabhāve'nyabhāvāḥ || 59 ||
taiścadhiṃ kālacakraḥ prakaṭayati mahānekanirmāṇakārya- vajrajvālāsphuradbhistvasurasuranṛṇāṃ kāmadhāto sthitānām |
saṃbhogai rūpiṇāṃ ve nabhasi jinasutādyarhatāṃ dharmakāryaḥ śūnyāṃśaiḥ śūnyakṛtsnaṃ tribhuvanasakalaṃ vāyubhirvāyukṛtsnam || 60||
tejo'śairvahnikṛtsnaṃ hyadakamapi jagatsphāritaiścodakāṃśaiḥ pṛthvīkṛtsnaṃ dharāśeḥ samuditaviṣayeḥ sarvavastusvabhāvam |
ekātmānaṃ samantād gaganasamamidaṃ darśayecchuddhabhūmyā- mevaṃ buddhasya kāyo bhavati na mriyate'pyeka saukhyasvabhāvāt || 61 ||
pañcākārāt tadekāt sakalajinavaraiścābhisambodhireṣā viṃśatyākārarūpā punarapi ca tato'nekamāyāsvarūpā |
tasyā ekakṣaṇā syāt samasukhaphaladā nānyakarmasvabhāvā atropāyo'cyuto yaḥ kṣaṇa iha sahajo dharmadhātupraveśe || 62 ||
buddhakṣetrāṇyanantānyaparimitaguṇā dhātavaścāmbarādyāḥ sthityutpattī vināśastrividha iti bhavaḥ ṣaḍgatau sarvasattvāḥ |
buddhāḥ krodhāḥ surādyāḥ sakaruṇahṛdayā bodhisattvāḥ sabhāryā etaccakraṃ jinasya tribhuvananamitasyaikamekasya śambhoḥ || 63 ||
'ato'ṣṭacatvāriṃśadvṛttamārabhya trayaṣṣaṣṭimaṃ vṛttaṃ yāvat ṣoḍaśaṃ vṛttāni subodhāni || 48-63 ||
yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ śarīre daśākāraśuddhirucyate- lomatvagraktamāṃsaṃ sarasamapi tathāsthīni majjā ca nāḍya- stāsu prāṇādivātā raviśaśigamanaṃ vāyumadhye samantāt |
vijñānaṃ candramadhye vimalamaṇirivāliṅgitaṃ sarvabhāve- rbhāvābhāvādvayatvaṃ paramamṛtapadaṃ vyāpakānāhataṃ tat ||64||

lometyādi |
iha loma keśa ākāśadhātuḥ, tvag māṃsaṃ vāyudhātuḥ, raktaṃ dvidhā tejo- dhātuḥ, svedaṃ mūtraṃ toyadhātu:, asthi majjā pṛthvīdhātuḥ, nāṭyaḥ prāṇādayo jñānadhātuḥ |
rāhupucchaṃ jñānavajram, rāhuścittavajram, ravirvāgvajram, candraḥ kāyavantram |
eṣāṃ caturṇāṃ prāṇamadhye gamanaṃ samantāt |
vijñānaṃ candramadhye śukramadhye, vimalamaṇiriva sphaṭika iva, māliṅgitaṃ sarvabhāvairupadhāyogataḥ |

śarīre daśākāraśuddhiḥ || 64 ||
idānīṃ daśabhūmibhiste dhātavaḥ śodhyante- lomādyāścandramo'ntāḥ sakalatanugatā bhūmayo yā daśoktāḥ ṣaḍvargā lādayo'nyāḥ samasukhaphaladā yogināṃ sarvakālam |
tasmādanveṣaṇīyā gurucaraṇagatairyogibhirmokṣaheto- rnānyo buddho'sti kaścitvapi tanuvirahād vyāpako mokṣado yaḥ || 65 ||
lomādyā iti |
iha loma keśāḥ pramuditā, carma māṃsaṃ vimalā, raktaṃ dviṣā prabhākarī, rasasalilamaciṣmatī, asthi majjā sudurjayā, nāḍī prāṇo'bhimukhī, kālāgnī rāgadhāturdūraṅgamā, rāhuracalā, raviḥ sādhumatī, candro dharmameghā, rāgata (sta)- morajaḥ sarakhadharmeṇa saṃvṛtyāvatāraḥ |
evaṃ lomādyāścandramohantāḥ sakalatanugatā nirā- varaṇagatāḥ|
teṣāṃ daśabhūmayastā daśoktā yā bhūmayaḥ, tāḥ punaḥ ṣaḍvargā lāvayo nyāsamātrikā uktāḥ |
tatra kavargaḥ pramuditā |
evaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ ca ṭa pa ta sa iti ṣaḍvargāḥ, la va ra ya ha al o ar e a la u ṛ i a ha: amiti |
evaṃ vilomenāpi dīrghādi- vargāḥ prajñābhūmayaḥ |
amī samasukhaphaladā yogināṃ sarvakālam |
tasmādanveṣaṇīyā guru- caraṇagatairavadhūtīcā ragatairyogibhirmokṣahetoriti bhagavato niyamaḥ |
nānyo buddho'sti kaścidapi tanuvirahād vyāpako mokṣado ya iti |
iha śarīradhātavo nirāvaraṇā buddhatva- dāyakā mokṣadāśceti || 65 ||
sattvā buddhā na buddhastvapara iha mahān vidyate lokadhātau tvaparimitabhavaśchidyate teṣāmārādhanena ca. traidhātavaḥ |
nirvikalpāt |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- drohaṃ kurvan hi yogī vrajati hi narakaṃ rauravādyaṃ mahāntaṃ tasmāccitte viśuddhe'pyabudhabudhajanānāṃ viruddhaṃ na kuryāt ||66||
labdhvā sattvaprasaṅgaṃ bhavati narapate yadvā saṃsāriṇāṃ syādakuśala guṇināṃ śuddhametryādicittaṃ dveṣarāgādicittam |
gacchantaṃ gantukāmaṃ dvividhamapi bhavet sarvadā bodhicittaṃ mokṣa prasthānahīnaṃ praṇidhivirahitaṃ sarvadā māracittam || 67||
mārāḥ kurvantyaśānti tribhuvananilaye bodhisattvāśca śānti mārendraścaddhihīnaḥ paramabhayakaraścaddhimānekaśāstā |
mārāṇāṃ mārabuddhiḥ parahṛdayagatā tāyināṃ saukhyabuddhi- stasmād buddhānubhāvaistribhuvanasakalaṃ vartate'nantakālam || 68 ||
yanmānaṃ lokadhātoḥ kathitamapi jinestannṛṇāṃ jānatāṃ na
buddhānāṃ nāsti mānaṃ sahajatanuvaśādekahastaṃ hyanekam
mānaṃ sattvānurūpaṃ prakaṭayati sadā prāṇināṃ karmabhūmā- vicchāmānaṃ tvidaṃ me yadi vadati surā nāstiko'yaṃ vadanti || 69 ||
yenotpannā jinendrāḥ pratidivasavaśānnirgatā yena garbhāt siddhā yena kṣaṇena kṣaraṇavirahitā spandaniḥspandabhūtāḥ
tyaktvā tad buddhakṛtyaṃ samasukharahitaṃ bhāvayed yo'nyaśūnyaṃ buddhatvaṃ tasya dūraṃ sahajamapi sukhaṃ koṭikalpairanekaiḥ || 70 ||
na drākṣā nimbavṛkṣādamṛtamapi viṣāt paṅkajaṃ brahmavṛkṣāt śūnyānnirvāṇa saukhyaṃ śubhamaśubhavaśāt siddhayaḥ prāṇighātāt
yajñāt svargaḥ paśūnāṃ paramaśivapadaṃ nendriyāṇāṃ nirodhād vedāt sarvajñabhāṣā'kṣarasukhamacalaṃ na kṣarāśuddhacittāt ||71||
sattvānāṃ pāpacittaṃ bhavati narapate'dhiṣṭhitaṃ mārakāyaiḥ puṇyajñānānuraktaṃ sukhadamapi sadādhiṣṭhitaṃ bodhisattvaḥ
nirvāṇaṃ yānti yasmāt sukhasamayavaśāt kleśamārānnihatya tasmāt kurvanti mārāḥ pratidinasamaye'neka vighnāni teṣām ||72||
sevyā''do karmamudrā jinasahajasukhasyāsya vṛddhayarthaheto- tanumukhacaraṇoṣṇīṣasarvāṅgapūrṇā
lakṣaṇāṅgaprapūrṇā paṭale, 66-76 ilo. ] yoginī tantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ stasmādādityarūpā vidyuddaṇḍānurūpā'cyutasukha jananī vajrarudbhāsayantī tribhava gatatanurdharmadhātustataḥ sarvasmin sarvakālaṃ syāt ||73||
etā mudrāścatasro'kṣarasukhaphaladā yoginā bhāvanīyā: surataratigaterloka māgaṃ prayukteḥ
grāmāraṇyaśmaśāne'śuciśucinilaye veśmadevālaye ca varṇāvarṇābhicāraistanubalasukhaderannapānādibhogeḥ ataḥ pañcaṣaṣṭivṛttādaparāṇi nava vṛttāni subodhāni catuḥsaptati- paryantamiti || 66-74 ||
idānīṃ śūnyatābimbe dṛṣṭe sati prāṇanirodhena yogināṃ kṣaṇalābha ucyate- vātaiḥ saṃghaṭṭamānaistaḍidanalaśikhā drāvayed mūrdhni candra yo yo bindudruto'smād galahṛdayagato nābhiguhye niruddhaḥ
bindoḥ spandadravaṃ yat kuliśamaṇigataṃ sanniruddhaṃ dhvajāgre prajñājñānakṣaṇaṃ tad yadi dadati sukhaṃ bindumālācyutena || 75 ||
vātairityādi
prāṇāyāmena daśavātānāṃ saṃghaṭṭanaṃ bhavati
prāmbimbe dṛṣṭe sati taiḥ prāṇāpānādibhirdaśabhiḥ saṃghaṭṭamānairnābhikaṇikāyāṃ caṇḍālī taḍidanalastasya śikhā raśmiḥ, sā śikhā mūni lalāṭe drāvayeccandramiti bodhicittaṃ janmasthānīyam
yo yo bodhicittavindudruto'smād lalāṭād gale hṛdaye nābhau guhye gato niruddho bhavati, prāṇāpānabalena kāryābindurguhye niruddhaḥ, evaṃ vākcittajñānabindurnābho hṛdaye kaṇṭhe niruddhaḥ, asmād bindoḥ spanvadravaṃ yaditi
iha guhyagatasya kāryābindorhyavaruddhasya yadaparaṃ svacchadravaṃ spandadravaṃ taducyate
tatra kuliśa maṇigataṃ sanniruddhaṃ dhvajāgre liṅgamukhe
prajñājñānakṣaṇaṃ spandaṃ tad yadi vadati sukhaṃ bindumālācyutena hetunā, tadā na karmamudrājñānam
prajñājñānamiti siddham || 75 ||
tasmānniḥspandasaukhyakṣaṇamiha sahajaṃ dharmadhāturdadāti prāṇenākṛṣya sarvān rasagatiṣu gatān kleśamārānnihatya
ṛddhi sarvajñabhūmi tribhuvanagurutāṃ yogināṃ janmanīha mṛtyuṃ mārgapraviṣṭo vrajati yadi tadā tadgrahādanyajātyā || 76 ||
tasmādacyutāddhetoryaniṣpanvasaukhyakṣaṇamiha dharmadhātuḥ prajñāpāramitā dadāti
tena mahāmudrāsukhaṃ paramākṣaraṃ karmajñānamudrāsukhaṃ spandamupāyasukhaṃ kṣaraṃ spandaṃ bālaṃ prauḍhaṃ ceti
yathā raso bālo yuvā vṛddho baddhaśceti, evaṃ śukramapi bālaṃ prauḍhaṃ vṛddhaṃ niruddhaṃ sarvāvaraṇakṣayataḥ
āvaraṇaṃ mala iti
tadeva bodhicittamūrdhvaṃ yadā yāti lalāṭe tadā yathāgataṃ tathāgataṃ bhavati
taccittaṃ prāṇenākṛṣya sarvān dvayayutadvayaṣṭaśatān rasagatiṣu gatān pṛthivyādiṣaṇmaṇḍalagatān
evaṃ kleśamārānnihatya ṛddhīḥ sarvajñasya dvādaśa bhūmorvakṣyamāṇāḥ, tribhuvanagurutāmiti sarvajñatā sarvākārajñatā mārgajñatā-mārgā- kārajñatādivibhūtīrdadāti yogināṃ janmanīha vīryavatām
ataḥ prāptayogo yadā mṛtyuṃ yāti māgaṃpraviṣṭaḥ san tadā tadgraheṇa vāsanābalena punarmanuṣyajātyā anyayā sarva prāpnoti, saptajanmaparyantenāvīryavānapi san jñānamabhāvayanniti niyamaḥ || 76 ||
idānīṃ bodhisatvādhiṣṭhānotpāda ucyate- yā nairguṇyālpabhāvā viṣayavirahitā kāyamudrā dvidhā sā tasyā rāgānuraktā guṇanidhiraparā vāksvarūpā dvidhā ca
kṛṣṇā śvetā'nuraktā viṣayaguṇaratā cittamudrā dvidhā syād aṣṭo dvo pañca pañca sphuṭamapi khayugaṃ śūnyavedaṃ ca varṣam ||77||
yā nairguṇyetyādi
iha yā kumārī nairguṇyā dharmadhātuguṇarahitā'cyavanāt, sā'lpabhāvā kāyadhātva pūrṇā'patitadantā ekā aṣṭavarṣāvidhiḥ
tato dvitīyā patitadantā daśavarṣāvadhikā
evaṃ kāyamudrā akṣatayoniḥ
tadavadherekādaśavarṣamārabhya pañcadaśavarṣaṃ yāvad ekā rāgānuraktā, guṇanidhiraparā'parapañcavarṣāṇi yāvad vāṅmudrā dvidhā syāt
evamaṣṭo varṣāṇi dvau saṃvatsarau, tataḥ pañca pañca vatsarāḥ
tato viśadvarṣādūrdhvamaparaṃ khaghugamiti catvāriṃśadvarṣāṇi
evaṃ cittamudrā ekā kṛṣṇabhāvānuraktā
evaṃ ṣaṣṭivārṣika strī
punaraparā śūnyavedamiti catvāriṃśad varṣaṃ yāvat śvetabhāvā- nuraktā vṛddhā jarāpalitavatī varṣaśataparyantam
evaṃ cittamudrāpi dviṣeti
āsāṃ tisṛṇāṃ madhye kāyamudrātibālā, vāṅmudrātiproḍhā, cittamudrātivṛddhā
etāstisro varjayitvā ekādaśavārṣikā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇā rakṣaṇīyā rājaguruṇā rājñā vā abhiṣiktena, anyairvā īśvarairabhiṣiktaiḥ || 77 ||
ratneśo yāvada-syājjinavarajananī yogibhī rakṣaṇīyā ratneśodbhūtakāle sakalaguṇanidhi maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā
yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ buddhādhiṣṭhānamantreḥ sṛjati samasukhaṃ guhyapadma samantraṃ guhye rakṣāṃ prakṛtya kuliśamaṇigataṃ svādayed bodhicittam ||78||
ratneśo yāvada- syāt
asyāditi na bhavati rajo yāvad rakṣaṇīyā
ratneśo- dabhūtakāle sakalaguṇanidhi maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā kālacakram, pūrvaṃ suśikṣitāṃ kṛtvā tatra tāmabhiṣicya buddhādhiṣṭhānamantraiḥ ṣaḍvacairmañjuśrīsamādhinā tāmapi prajñāṃ niṣpādya sṛjati samasukhaṃ dakṣiṇanāḍīpravāhakāle bodhicittam, tasyā: guhyapadme samantraṃ kāyavā cittajñānamantrasahitaṃ sphārayet
tatastasyā guhye rakṣāṃ prakṛtya ṣaḍaṅgaistataḥ svakuliśamaṇigataṃ bodhicittamāsvādayet
taṃ yogī tasyā api dadāti
tasyāḥ padmatrāhyaṃ yattadanāmikāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ gṛhītvā āsvādayet sarvarakṣārthamiti, anena vidhinā || 78 ||
tasmin putro bhaved yo jinajanakasuto majuvajraḥ sa eva prajñādhikyāt kadācit prabhavati duhitā buddhamātā dhruvaṃ syāt
tasmādanyestripuṣpaiḥ sitakamaladharo jambhalo vajrapāṇi- ranye'ṣṭāvaṣṭapuṣpaidiśi vidiśigatā maharddhikāḥ kodharājāḥ || 79 ||
tasminnādhāne ya: putro bhavati mañjuvajñaḥ sa eva prajñādhikyāditi raja ādhikyāda yadi duhitā bhavati, tadā viśvamātādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati
tena buddhamātā dhruvaṃ
seti mañjuśrī viśvamātā'dhiṣṭhānavidhiḥ
tasmādanyaistripuṣpairiti
iha yadi prathamarajasi garbho na jātaḥ, tadā punaḥ punaḥ pratyekarajodarśanena maṇḍalaṃ vartayitvā'parasamādhibhi- bodhicittamutsṛjediti
evaṃ dvitīye sitakamalaghara iti lokeśvaranirmāṇaṃ bhavati
tṛtīye jambhalanirmāṇam, caturthe 'vajrapāṇiḥ putraviṣaye
duhitāviṣaye pāṇḍarāvasudhārā- śabdavatrādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati
tato'nye 'ṣṭāvaṣṭapuṣpaidiśiviviśigatā mahaddhikāḥ krodharājā iti
iha pūrva jambhalādhiṣṭhāne uṣṇīṣasamādhinoṣṇīṣādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati
atinīlāyā vāgvajrapāṇisthāne śumbharājo vā raudrākṣī vā bhavati
tataḥ pañcame rajasi vighnānta- ko'nantavīryā, ṣaṣṭhe prajñāntako jambho, saptame padmāntako māninī, aṣṭame yamāntakaḥ stambhīti
tato navame mahābalo māre (rī)cī, daśame'calaścundā, ekādaśe ṭakkirbhṛkuṭī, dvādaśe nīladaṇḍo vajraśṛṅkhaleti vidikṣu
evaṃ krodhasamādhinā krodhādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati garbhasye ti
tatastrayodaśamo rajaḥ punaḥ prathamo yathā avidyā- dyaṅgam || 79 ||
tasmin māse rajo yat prabhavati hi punamaṃjughoṣodbhavaṃ tad viśadvarṣāṇi yāvat pratirajasi mahāsāttviko bodhisattvaḥ
kiñcit sattvāṃśahīnaḥ prabhavati ca tataḥ śūnyavedāni yāvad viśadvarṣāṇi yāvat punarapi ca tataścānyasattvo'lpavīryaḥ || 80 ||
tena tasmin māse rajo yat prabhavati hi punarmañjughoṣodbhavaṃ tadrajaḥ
evaṃ tadupari yaṃ samādhimālambayitvā yogī bodhicittaṃ visarjayet, tadadhiṣṭhānaṃ garbhasya bhavati viṃśadvarṣāṇi yāvat pratirajasi yadā bodhisattvo bhavati, eko vā ho vā ityādi, mātumahāsāsviko bhavati
kiñcit satvāṃśahīno bhavati catvāriṃśadvarṣāṇi yāva- nmātuviśativarṣāṇi yāvat
punarapi ca tataścānyasa svo'lpavīryaḥ
evamaśītivarṣāṇi mātuḥ sattvarajastamobhedena garbhasyādhiṣṭhānam || 80 ||
tasmād yonī rajo na pratihataviṣayastadvināśānna seka: prajñābhiṣeko jinapativacanenaṃṣṭabījasya deyaḥ
tāsāṃ bhūtoyatejo'nilagagana guṇānveṣaṇīyā jināṅga - reṣā sattvārthakartrī bhavati bahuphalā bodhicittasya sevā || 81 ||
tasmādyonau rajo na pratihata garbhaviṣayaḥ, tadvināśāna sekaḥ prajñāyā upāyasya ca bījābhāve - bījamiti prarohāt yatkintu jñābhiṣeko jinapativacanairnaṣṭabojasya vṛddhasya deyo yathā bhikṣoḥ, tathā prajñāyāmapi yathā bhikṣuṇyāḥ
evaṃ viṃśadvārṣikāḥ śobhanāḥ sekārthamadhiṣṭhānārthaṃ bhūdhāturviṃśativiṃśativarṣaistathaiva toyatejo'nilagaganaguṇā varṣaśataṃ yāvajjanāṅgairanveṣaṇīyā iti
eṣā sattvārthakartrī bhavati bahuphalā bodhicittasya sevā bodhisattvotpādanata iti || 81 ||
idānīmamṛtapānamucyate- yā'sṛkpānaṃ karoti pravarasuranṛṇāṃ makṣikā sā'mṛtekā chadirguhyādivaktrāt kuliśamaṇigataṃ nābjamadhye praviṣṭam
viṇmūtraṃ raktamāṃsaṃ paramasamarasaṃ chardimadhye praviṣṭa- metajjñānāmṛtaṃ ca tribhuvanapatinā deśitaṃ sarvatantre ||82||
yā'sṛk pānamityādi
iha bāhye neyārthena yā'sṛkpānaṃ karoti makṣikā, sā nītārthenāvadhūtī
yā cyavanakāle rajodhātuṃ pibati pravarasuranṛṇāṃ sā makṣikā'mṛtā, ekā'vadhūtī, ruddhetyarthaḥ
yā bāhye bhuktacchaviḥ sā nītārthena chardirbodhicittam, guhyāvivaktrāt kuliśamaṇimataṃ na bāhye prajñābjamadhye praviṣṭam
evamacyutaṃ viṇmūtraṃ raktamāṃsaṃ paramasamarasam aśrāvaṃ chadimadhye praviṣṭaṃ niruddhaṃ nirāvaraṇaṃ bhavati
eta- jjñānāmṛtaṃ syādacyutaṃ pañcāmṛtaṃ tribhuvana 'patinā deśitaṃ sarvatantre, na bāhye viṣṭhādikaṃ siddhaye deśitamityamṛta niyamaḥ || 82 ||
idānīṃ bāhye samayarakṣaṇabhakṣaṇamucyate- prajñādharmodaye yat patitamapi sukhaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnād yaḥ kaścittena sattvo bhavati jinakule bodhisattvaḥ sa eva
tasmāt taṃ bhakṣayanti pratidinasamaye rākṣasā mārabhūtā: prajñāpuṣpeṇa yuktaṃ śivasukhaphaladaṃ bhakṣitaṃ deśayanti ||83 ||
prajñetyādi
iha samāpattī bhāvanāṃ kurvato yogino'yantritaṃ bodhicittaṃ yadi prajñādharmodaye patati, tadā prajñādharmodaye patitamapi sukhaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnāt
tatkasya hetoḥ ?
yaḥ kaścittena bodhicittena samādhinotsṛṣṭena bhavati, sa jinakule bodhisattva eva sugatavaṃśapravardhano yasmāt tasmāt taṃ bodhicittaṃ saugatānāṃ rakṣārahitamasamāhitānāṃ pratidinasamaye bhakṣayanti mārabhūtā rākṣasāḥ śukrāhāriṇaḥ, yogivyapadeśena narā api prajñāpuṣpeṇa yuktaṃ śivasukhaphaladaṃ bhakṣitaṃ deśayanti
anyeṣāṃ bālajanānāṃ naraka- gamanāyeti || 83 ||
nākaṭyaṃ vajramabjāt paramasukhagataṃ saṃkucaṃ naiva yāvad buddhādhiṣṭhānamevaṃ prabhavati hi yadā kāmasiddhistadā vai
vajrābjābhyāṃ praviśya svakuliśahṛdaye jñānacakraṃ praviṣṭaṃ cakrākāraṃ karoti hyubhayatanumimāṃ raśmibhiḥ pūrayitvā || 84 ||
tato boddhamantriṇā nākaṭyaṃ vajramanjād yāvanna saṃkoco bhavati, buddhādhiṣṭhānamevaṃ prabhavati hi yadā kāma siddhistadā ve vajramārgeṇa upāyahṛdaye praviśya padmamārgeṇa prajñāhṛdaye praviśya 'svakuliśahṛdaye devatācakraṃ praviṣṭaṃ cakrākāraṃ karotyubhayatanumimāṃ raśmibhiḥ pūrayitveti || 84 ||
eṣā siddhiyaṃdi syānnahi kuliśamaṇau saṃsthitaṃ prajñādharmodayasthe sakalajinakulaṃ sthāpayed prajñāyukte tvartheke punarapi vacanaṃ procyate buddhakāyo yaḥ kaściccāsya nāśaṃ hyabhilaṣati śaṭho māryate vajriṇā saḥ || 85 ||
eṣā siddhinaṃhi syādyadi, tadā kuliśamaṇau saṃsthitaṃ bodhicittamanāmāṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ gṛhotvā bhakṣaṇīyam
evaṃ prajñāyāḥ[ [82b] padmabāhye āgataṃ tadeva dvābhyāmapi bhakṣaṇīyam
prajñādharmodayasthe punaḥ sakalajinakulaṃ sthāpayedrakṣaṇārthaṃ ṣaḍvatrāṇi lalāṭādiṣu hṛdayādiṣu ṣaḍaṅgamiti
prajñāyukta iti rajoyukte, arthake bodhicitte rakṣite mahatpuṇyaṃ bhavati
punarapi vacanaṃ procyate buddhakāya idaṃ bodhicittam
yaḥ kaścid mārakāyiko'sya nāśa- mabhilaṣati śaṭho māryate vajriṇā sa herukeṇeti bodhicittasya samayarakṣaṇabhakṣaṇa- niyamaḥ || 85 ||
ye proktā'nekamantrāstribhuvanapatinā krūrakarmasvabhāvā- ste sarve mārapakṣakṣayabhayajanakāḥ prāṇināṃ no kadācit
kartāro ye smṛtīnāṃ raṇaviṣayaratā mārakānye'pi tīrthyā- steṣāṃ te yojanīyāḥ paramajinasuteḥ prāṇināṃ rakṣaṇārtham || 86 ||
iha mantranaye ye proktā aneka mantrāstribhuvanapatinā krūrakarmasvabhāvāḥ, te sarve mārapakṣakṣayabhayajanakāḥ prāṇināṃ no kadācit
atra mārāḥ kartāro ye smṛtīnāṃ raṇa- viṣayaratā mārakā anye'pi tīrthikāḥ teṣāṃ te yojanīyāḥ paramajinasutaiḥ prāṇināṃ rakṣaṇā- thaṃm
teṣāṃ pakṣa iti , tasya dharmasya kṣayabhayahetoḥ, na teṣāṃ prāṇahānaye || 86 ||
ḍākinyo vajrapūrvāḥ paśujananidhane'dhyeṣaṇīyā na vidbhiḥ sattvānāṃ rakṣaṇārthaṃ tribhuvanapatinā sphāritā lokaghātī
tasmāt tā rakṣayanti pratidinasamaye sādhakaṃ dveṣayanti sādhyaḥ karmaprabhāvād vrajati hi maraṇaṃ sādhakasyaiṣa māraḥ ||87 ||
iha yoginotantre yā vajraḍākinyastāḥ paśujanānāmajñānināṃ māraṇārthaṃ nādhyeṣaṇīyā bauddhavidvadbhiḥ
kutaḥ ?
yatastāḥ sattvānāṃ rakṣaṇārthaṃ tribhuvana 'patinā sphāritā lokadhātau
tasmāt tā rakṣayanti lokān
paramakāruṇikāḥ sādhakaṃ dveṣayanti lokavisaṃvādakam, atha māraṇādikarmaṇi kṛte sati yadi sādhyaḥ karmaprabhāvād vrajati hi maraṇam, tadā sādhakasyaiṣa māro mayā hata ityahaṅkārataḥ sādhako narakaṃ yāti kṣudrakarmaṇeti nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ kṣudrakarma na karoti, yathā ātmānaṃ tathā paśyati sattvān ||87||

paṭale, 85-89 ślo. ] yoginītantrādispharaṇa mahoddeśaḥ kṣudravidyānuraktaṃ puṃsāṃ cittaṃ samantādaśubhaphalavaśāt vidyeyaṃ me karoti tribhuvanasakalaṃ vaśyamekakṣaṇena
ityāśālubdhacittaḥ praviśati narakaṃ jñānadānaṃ vihāya pṛṣṭhe dravyaṃ svabhāryā bhavati paravaśā'ho kudharmapravṛttiḥ || 88 ||
iha saṃsāriṇāṃ puṃsāṃ cittaṃ samantādaśubhaphalavaśāt kṣudravidyānuraktam
vidyeyaṃ me karoti tribhuvanasakalaṃ vaśyamekakṣaṇenetyāśālubdhacittaḥ praviśati narakaṃ jñānadānaṃ vihāya maraṇamupagataḥ
pṛṣṭhe maraṇamupagate sati paravaśaṃ dravyam, svabhāryā paravaśā bhavati anyā- saktā
aho saṃsāriṇāṃ kudharmapravṛttiḥ || 88 ||

sahajatanuriyaṃ dharmaṃkāyo babhūva na prajñā nāpyupāyaḥ sahajatanuriyaṃ prajñopāyasvarūpaḥ khalu vigatatamo jñānavijñānabhedāt
so'yaṃ saṃbhogakāyaḥ pratiravaka ivānekasattvārthakartā sattvānāṃ pākahetorbhavati punaraso buddhanirmāṇakāyaḥ || 89 ||
na prajñetyādi
iha prajñā pañcadaśakalātmaka: śuklapakṣaḥ, kṛṣṇapakṣaścandrakalā- hānirupāyaḥ
evaṃ śuklo rātriḥ kṛṣṇo divā
ataḥ sahajakāyo na prajñā nāpyupāyaḥ sahajatanuriyaṃ buddhānām
evaṃ na sacchuklapakṣaḥ, nāsatkalābhāvaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ, na sadasad anayoḥ parasparavirodhato melāpako nāsti
na cāpyanubhayātmakamiti na cābhyāṃ śuklakṛṣpakṣābhyāṃ vinā tat sahajasukham

ca. prajñetyādinā
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- dharmiṇī sahajatanurucyate, niḥṣyandalakṣaṇāttūryākṣayato yoginām
evaṃ napuṃsakamiti siddham
iha sahajatanuḥ svārthasampat parārthasampade dharmakāyo [1832 ] babhūva suṣuptakṣayataḥ
sa ca prajñopāyasvarūpaḥ khalu vigatatamo jñānavijñānabhedāditi
iha jñānaṃ grāhakacittaṃ yoginaḥ, vijñānaṃ paracittajñānaṃ grāhyaṃ jñeyalakṣaṇam
evaṃ grāhakacittaṃ prajñā kalpanārahitatvāt, yogināmupāyo 'grāhyacittaṃ parikalpitaṃ karuṇālakṣaṇam
tena grāhaka grāhyabhedena prajñopāya- svarūpaḥ parārthakartā dharmakāyaḥ
sa ca sahajād babhūveti
evaṃ niṣyando nābhau sahajaḥ, dharmacakre hṛdaye vipākaḥ, so'yaṃ dharmakāyaḥ saṃbhogakāyaḥ parārthasampade pratirabaka ivāneka- satvārthakartā
iha divyacakṣuṣā yadatītānāgataṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ pratibimbākāraṃ svaccham, tasmin śabdo yo niścarati sa pratiśabdaḥ sambhogakāyaḥ prajñopāyasvarūpaḥ
divyaśrotreṇa divyavijñānaṃ grāhakam, pratiśabdo grāhyaḥ
tenātītānāgatakālasaṃkhyāṃ jānāti - amuka kalpe'mukayuge'mukavarṣe'mukamāse'mukapakṣe'mukadinādike'muko bhūtaḥ amuko bhaviṣya- tīti
tena sattvā veneyā iti svapnāvasthākṣayataḥ kaṇṭhe puruṣakāraḥ sambhogakāya ūrdhvaretasaḥ
sattvānāṃ pākahetorbhavati punarasau sambhogakāyo nirmāṇakāyo bhavati prajñopāyātmakaḥ
eko'pi sattvānāṃ nānānirmāṇadarśanato'nekaḥ
evamekānekayoryogo vivṛtyā prajñopāyaḥ saṃvṛtyā ekānekavirodhaḥ
sa ca jāgradavasthākṣayato lalāṭe vaimalyo nirmāṇakāyaḥ aśeṣarūpa sandarśī ratna ketu mahāmaṇi: ( nā0 saṃ0 9.24 ) iti
evamekaḥ sahajaḥ, sa eva dharmaḥ sambhogo nirmāṇaśceti caturdheti || 89 ||
eko'sau vajrasattvaḥ pralayaghananibho heruko ve babhūva raudrāṇāṃ pācanārthaṃ sa ca samayajino mohitānāṃ sukhārtham
ratneśo duḥkhitānāṃ sa ca kamaladharo rāgiṇāṃ rāgaheto- vighnānāṃ dhvaṃsanāthaṃ tvasikarakamalo'moghasiddhirbabhūva ||90||
sa eva pūrvokta eko'sau vajrasattvaḥ pralayaghananibhaḥ kṛṣṇo heruko vaṃ babhūva
acyutadharmeṇānyat tadvijñānaṃ herukaḥ
sa ca raudrāṇāṃ pācanārthaṃ vajrasattvaspharaṇamiti
sa ca vajrasattvo mohitānāṃ pācanārthaṃ samayajino vairocano babhūva, anyat tadrūpam
sa ca ratneśo duḥkhitānāṃ dānārthaṃ ratnasambhavo babhūva, acyutadharmeṇānyā sā vedanā sarva- duḥkhāpahantrīti
sa ca kamalagharo rāgiṇāṃ rāgaheto ramitābho babhūva, acyutadharmeṇā- nyā sā saṃjñā yā acyutasukhadātrīti
sa ca vighnānāṃ dhvaṃsanāthaṃ svasikarakamalo - moghasiddhirbabhūva, acyutadharmeṇānye te saṃskārāḥ, ye nirāvaraṇaṃ cittaṃ kurvanti, mārādyā- varaṇaṃ vidhvaṃsa yantīti pañcabuddhaviśuddhā iti || 90 ||

iha yat kleśādyāvaraṇarahitānāṃ garbhāvakramaṇaṃ pañcaskandhagrahaṇaṃ vayoṣiṣu strīgarbhe sambhavāya viharaṇam, tat kṛpayā avihitaniyamānāṃ prākṛta- janānāmapuṇyājitānāṃ pācanāya |

idaṃ devatvaṃ cyavanakāle mahad duḥkhamiti deśanayā teṣāṃ pācanam |

tiyaṃ pretāsurāṇāmuragasuranṛṇām āryabhoṭādikānāṃ bhūteṣyadvartamānaṃ trividhamapi sadā satyadharmaṃ bruvantī |

dānādyāḥ ṣaṭ catasraḥ samasukhaphaladāḥ śaktayastā daśoktā- stāsāṃ śuddhā upāyāḥ paramadaśavidhāḥ pūrṇacittā ghaṭānte |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- mārakleśakṣayā vai madana iti haro vajravṛndraṃ drumādyāḥ ṣaṇmudrā: śūnyaṣaṭkaṃ tribhuvanajanako jñānavijñānamekam ||100||
cakraṃ svacchaṃ samantāt tribhava iti sukhaṃ ratnamasyaiva rāgaḥ padmaṃ kleśakṣayo'si: kuliśamapi mahājñānakāyo hyabhedyaḥ |
chedo'jñānasya kartrī tviha ' ṣaḍapi kulānyebhirutpāditā ye te'pyevaṃ veditavyāḥ khamiva samarasāḥ skandhadhātvindriyādyāḥ || 101 ||
yasmin vai jātirūpaṃ vrajati nidhanatāṃ tanmahārūpamuktaṃ yasyāṃ saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ vrajati nidhanatāṃ sā mahāvedanoktā |

yasmin jāgrādyavasthā vrajati nidhanatāṃ tacca vijñānamuktaṃ yasminnajñānabhāvo vrajati nidhanatāṃ tanmunerjñānameva |
ete vairocanādyā: paramajinavarā: ṣaḍvidhāḥ ṣaṭkulāni anye ṣaḍdhātubhedā avaniśikhipayomārutākāśaśāntāḥ || 103 ||

catuḥ kāyādiśuddhi nirṇaya mahoddeśaḥ vajrasparśaṃ samantādapaharati sadā vajrakāyendriyaṃ tat prajñopāyaprasaṅgād viśati samasukhe yo manaḥ so'sya dharmaḥ || 106 ||
ete ṣaḍbhedabhinnā viṣayaviṣayiṇo bodhisattvāḥ sabhāryā viśve rūpādayo'mī jinavara viṣayāḥ ṣaṭprakārāḥ samantāt |

iha kila maṇḍalacakrabhāvanāyāṃ samayasattvaṃ niṣpādya tato jñāna- cakrasyākarṣaṇaṃ praveśanaṃ bandhanaṃ toṣaṇaṃ samarasaṃ kartavyaṃ mantriṇā |
jaḥ hūṃ vaṃ horiti vajrāṅkaśenākarṣaṇaṃ vajreṇa praveśanaṃ vajrapāśena bandhanaṃ ghaṇṭayā toṣaṇaṃ pūrvadakṣiṇa-
paścimottaradvāreṣu sthiteḥ krodharājeryoginītantre vajraḍākinībhiḥ sarvatra 'kīrtitam

yuvatīprasaṅge jñānasattvaḥ śukraṃ sandhyābhāṣāntareṇa tasyākarṣaṇaṃ prāṇādivāyuvṛnda bhedaṃ karotyūrdhve śirasi paripūrṇaṃ karoti bodhicittamityarthaḥ
tatpraveśaṃ ca jambha iti tejastadbhāva- yitvā dravasya bindurūpasya kaṇṭhe hṛdaye nābhau guhyakamale praveśaṃ karotīti
stambha iti pṛthvīdhātustasya bindurūpasya bandhanaṃ karotyāgatasyātivegena gantuṃ na dadāti paramasukhavaśāviti
mānakastoyadhātubindoḥ svacchadravaṃ guhyakamale vajramaṇī gataṃ spandamityucyate, tasyācyutasukhavaśāttoyadhātustoṣaṇaṃ śarīre śaityaṃ karotītyarthaḥ
punastatsthānādūrdhvagamanārthaṃ tadeva niḥṣyandasukhaṃ locanādayaḥ prabodhayanti vajragīti- kābhiḥ pūrvoktābhiḥ
tato nābhau niṣyandanārthaṃ locanā codayati, hṛdaye māmakī vipākārtham, kaṇṭhe pāṇḍarā puruṣakārārtham, śirasi tārā vaimalyārtham
evamādibhi- rutthāpito mahāsukho vaimalyaṃ gataḥ sarvaskandhadhātvāyatanādikaṃ nirāvaraṇaṃ karoti
tataḥ sarvajñapadaṃ yogināṃ bhavati, na kalpitamaṇḍale yoginīgīte notthāpita iti nītārtha: sarvatantreṣu saṃvṛtyā vivṛtyā ceti || 108 ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- mārāṇāṃ tāḍanaṃ ve tribhuvananilaye nīladaṇḍaḥ karoti bandhaṃ krodho'calaśca pralayara vinibhaḥ kīlanaṃ cātivīryaḥ
ṭakkistadvīryanāśaṃ tvavanitalagataṃ stambhanaṃ stambha eva uṣṇīṣaścaddhināśaṃ prakaṭitaniyatāḥ pūrvavat krodhadevyaḥ || 109 ||
pretānāṃ pācanārthaṃ sphuraṇamapi tataścacikā debaṃbhūva indrādīnāṃ surāṇāṃ diśi vidiśi tathā rakṣaṇārthaṃ janānām

nanu yadi rūpabhāvanā vikalpaḥ, vikalpādyogī buddho na bhaviṣyatīti tadā kimartha - midaṃ śarīraṃ maṇḍalacakradevatā gaṇairviśodhanīyamiti tathāgatavacanamiti kasyacidabhiprāyo bhaviṣyati ?
tasmāducyate - iha satyametat, samayabhāṣayā tathāgatena pratyekaṃ vairocanādikaṃ taduddiṣṭam, na punarghaṭapaṭaśabdavad vācyavācakabhā [ 1912 ] vena svarūpataḥ
strīndriyaṃ padmam, puruṣendriyaṃ vajtram, gūtho vairocanaḥ, mūtramakṣobhyaḥ, raktaṃ ratnasambhavaḥ, śukramamitābhaḥ, māṃsamamoghasiddhiḥ, kintu pañcatathāgatānāṃ nāmabhiḥ pañcāmṛtānāṃ saṃjñā uktā:, na punaḥ paramārthayuktito devatāḥ
tasmād devatākāyo vaktuṃ na śakyate pūtiśarīramidam, asāra- tvāditi

paṭale, ślo. ] paramākṣarajñāna siddhirnāma mahoddeśaḥ
atha dhyānamantrabalena nekabuddhakṣetralokadhātusthān sattvānākṛṣya purataḥ sthāpayitvā teṣāṃ dharmadeśanāṃ karoti
tān mārge sthāpayitvā svasvalokadhātau visarjayet
tadevāti- śayena viparītam, sarvāmbara kuhareṣvaneka lokadhātusthānāmasaṃkhyeyānāṃ sattvānāṃ rūpiṇāṃ paramāṇurūpeṇāpi purato'vasthānaṃ kartuṃ na śakyate
atha bālamatīnāṃ vacanamanenāpi rūpakāyenaikasmin buddhakṣetre trisāhasramahā sāhasralokadhātuṣu sthitānāṃ sattvānāmarthakriyāṃ karoti, etadvacanaṃ pāramārthikyā yuktyā vicāryamāṇaṃ nirarthakam, yatheśvaravacanamājñā- siddhaṃ yuktipramāṇarahitam
āptāgamādita īśvaro niṣkalaḥ sarvakartā
sa ca kārya - iha vairocanādīni pañcāmṛtāni gokudahanādipalāni bhakṣyāṇi svabhāvaśuddhāni tathāgate- oktāni ebhirbhakṣitaiḥ śarīramajarāmaraṃ bhaviṣyati, vajrasattvo'pi varado bhaviṣyatīti
anyatra vajrakule krodharājasamādhinā prāṇino ghātyāḥ khaṅgakule'moghasiddhisamādhinā- 'satyaṃ vaktavyam, ratnakule ratnasambhavasamādhinā parasvaṃ hāryam, padmakule'mitābha- samādhinā parastrī grāhyā, cakrakule vairocanasamādhinā pañcāmṛtapalāni bhakṣaṇīyānīti, apare'pi daśākuśalakarmapathā devatāyogena yoginā kartavyā iti, evaṃ duṣṭācāryavacanaṃ pramāṇīkṛtya daśākuśalān karmapathān kurvanti, aśodhitānyabodhitānyapradīpitānyana- mṛtīkṛtāni' bhakṣayanti
tāni ca bhakṣitāni pañcāmṛtāni na teṣāṃ bhakṣakāṇāṃ buddhatva- guṇadāyakāni bhavantīti, tathāgatavacanāprabodhatvāditi
nanu sarvatantrarājeṣu tathāgatenoktāni pañcāmṛtāni yoginā bhakṣaṇīyāni, iha kathameṣāṃ pratiṣedha: ?

tasmāducyate - iha yadvaktavyaṃ bālajanaiḥ pañcāmṛtādīni bhakṣyāṇi tathāgatenoktāni sarvatantrarājeṣu tatsatyam, kintu yogināṃ na prākṛtasattvānām
yeṣāṃ mantrabalena dhyānabalena vā śodhitāni bodhitāni pradīpitānyamṛtīkṛtāni viṣāṇi nirviṣāṇi bhavanti, madyāni kṣīrāṇi bhavanti, viṣoda- kādīni duṣṭāni sattvānāṃ maraṇa [ [92b]dāyakāni rasāyanāni bhavati, asthīni puṣpāṇi bhavanti, dantā muktāphalāni bhavanti, kapālaṃ padmaṃ bhavanti, māṃsaṃ putrakeśo bhavati, raktaṃ sihnakaṃ bhavati, mūtraṃ kastūrikā bhavati, śukraṃ karpūraṃ bhavati, gūthaṃ catuḥ samaṃ bhavati, lomāni kuṅkumakeśarāṇi bhavanti
evamanekaduṣṭadravyāṇi duṣṭasvabhāva parityāgāt śodhitāni bodhitāni pradīpitānyamṛtīkṛtāni svabhāvaśuddhāni tathāgatenoktāni na duṣṭasvabhāvā- parityāgāt
tāni ca viṣādoni svabhāvaśuddhānyamṛtīkṛtāni yogibhirbhakṣitāni śarīre mahābalapuṣṭikarāṇi bhavanti
gūthādīni svabhāvaśuddhāni śarīre vilepitāni divyagandha- mudvahanti
ato yogināṃ tathāgatenoktāni nānyeṣāṃ devatāmantrāsādhitānāṃ parṣanmūrkhāṇāṃ paṇḍitābhimānināṃ vakamāyādharāṇāṃ maṭhavihāradravyābhilāṣiṇāṃ pretanarakajātī janmo- tpādanibaddhānāṃ svārthaparārtha bhraṣṭānāṃ tantroktārthaviparītārthasaṃdeśa kānāmaparīkṣajanasya mahā- mārakāyikānāṃ bhakṣyāṇi bhagavatoktānīti
tairdaṣṭacittaistāni viṣādīni duṣṭadravyāṇi pañcāmṛtāni śodhitāni bodhitāni pradīpitānyamṛtīkṛtānyapyaneka kalpairduṣṭasvabhāvāni na parityajanti, duṣṭasvabhāvāparityāgāt svabhāvaśuddhāni na bhavantīti
tasmātteṣāṃ duṣṭa- dravyāṇi (ṇāṃ) dhyānabalena vā mantrabalena vā yāvad duṣṭasvabhāvaṃ hartuṃ na śaknoti kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā bhikṣurvā bhikṣuṇī vā upāsaka upāsikā vā ādikamiko vā vajra- tasya vajrācāryeṇa guruṇā ādikamikeṇa vā mantrasāmarthyayuktena vā yānarataḥ, paramākṣarajñāna siddhirnāma mahoddeśaḥ viṣādipañcāmṛtādibhakṣaṇavilepane niyamo na dātavyaḥ, aśuddhaviṣādibhakṣaṇānmaraṇaṃ bhavati, aśuddhasamaya vilepanāllokāvadhyānaṃ bhavati, svāghātamaraṇāllokāvadhyānānnarako bhavati, kumantriṇāṃ sattvāśayaparityāgādityuktaṃ nāmasaṃgotyāmadhyeṣaṇāyāṃ pañcadaśama- ślokena - prakāśayiṣye sattvānāṃ yathāśayaviśeṣataḥ

tasmāducyate - iha yadvaktavyaṃ bālajanaiḥ pramāṇaśāstrādikaṃ sarvaṃ viruddham, tanna, kasmāt ?
tatvaparīkṣācittānna teṣāṃ darśanānāṃ parasparavādināṃ doṣo'sti paramārthaparīkṣāśrita- citāt sarvasattvāpakāracittābhāvāt sarvasattvopakāracittāt
atra punarviṣādi - pañcāmṛtādibhakṣaṇavilepane lokavyavahāro na paramārthaparīkṣā
viṣādipañcāmṛtādikaṃ ca tattvaṃ na bhavati, yasya parīkṣamāṇā doṣā na bhaviṣyanti lokāvadhyānato'pi
tasmāt [193a] satvāśayavaśād dharmadeśanā bauddhānāṃ na sattvāśayavirahiteti
āśayo'pi sattvānāṃ deśa kula vyavahāreṇa bhavati, tasmātteṣāṃ deśakulavyavahāreṇa laukikasaṃvṛtisatyena laukika- dharmo deśanīyo vajrācāryeṇa

na ca bhakṣyābhakṣyagrahaṇaṃ bhavati

tasmādādikarmakeṇa 'svadeśavyavahāreṇa bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ kartavyaṃ kulākulābhigamanaṃ ca
kutracideśe kāpālikakapālodakena śuddhi- bhavati, kutraciddeśe gomāṃsa bhakṣaṇīyam, kutraciddeśe'śvamāṃsam, kutraciddeśe śuno māṃsama, kutraciddeśe hastimāṃsam kutraciddeśe naramāṃsa bhakṣaṇīyam

kutraciddeśe śvocchiṣṭaṃ bhakṣaṇīyam, kutraciddeśe bhojane kṛte nācamanam, kutraciddeśe śūkaramāṃsaṃ bhakṣaṇīyam, kutraciddeśe mahiṣamāṃsam, kutraciddeśe chāgalamāṃsam

kutraciddeśe bhartari mṛte sati putrasya mātā bhāryā bhavati, kutraciddeśe bhrātṛbhaginyovivāhaḥ, kutraciddeśe mātulakasambandhaḥ, kutraciddeśe kulākulānāṃ parasparābhigamanam kutraciddeśe brāhmaṇyo veśyāvyavahāraṃ kurvanti

paṭale, ślo
paramākṣarajñāna siddhirnāma mahoddeśaḥ ato bhagavato vacanāt pañcāmṛtabhakṣaṇād mantrajāpamaṇḍalacatra vikalpabhāvanā- balād yogī samyaksambuddho na bhavatīti
nanu bhagavatoktaṃ maṇḍalacakre sākṣātkṛte sati yogināṃ mahāmudrāsiddhirbhavati, tadiha kathaṃ maṇḍalacakre'pi sākṣātkṛte yogī sarvajño na bhavatītīha keṣāñcidabhiprāyo bhaviṣyati
tasmāducyate - iha pūrvamevoktaṃ maṇḍalacakraṃ vikalpajālaṃ prādeśikaṃ prādeśika siddhiphaladam
atha mūrkhāṇāṃ bhrāntiriyamamukasiddhiḥ karmamudrāṃ gṛhītvā, iha guṭikābalena mantrabalena saptāvartabalena khaḍgādibalena dhyānabalena vā maṇḍalacakra- sādhanabalena vā'dṛśyo bhavati, na sarvāvaraṇa vinirmuktaḥ sarvajño bhagavān vajrasattvo bhavatīti

| vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- tena kāraṇena sattvāśayavaśāt tathāgatena dharmo deśita: - vaibhāṣikaḥ sautrāntiko yogācāro madhyamakaśceti
teṣu vaibhāṣikamupapattyaṅgika satvaprakāśakaṃ śāstram, sūtrāntamarthaprakāśakaṃ śāstram, yogācāraṃ vijñānamātraprakāśakaṃ śāstram, madhyamakaṃ pāramārthikatattvaprakāśakaṃ śāstraṃ sattvāśayavaśāduktaṃ bhagavatā dūrā (ra) bhavyāsannabhavya- cittāt
iha sattvā anādikāle tīrthikā devabhūtāsuragharmaratāḥ sarvajñamārganaṣṭāścatu- varṇekavarṇāśritāḥ svargaphalopabhogābhilāṣiṇaḥ kartrātmavādinaḥ
teṣāṃ śabdavādino devapreta- dharmāśritā īśvaravādinaścātmavādinaśca jātivādinaśceti
mlecchā'suradharmāśritāḥ kartṛvādino jīvavādino jātivādarahitāḥ
eṣāṃ mlecchānāmubhayagrahaḥ – paramāṇu- sandohagrahaḥ, upapattyaṅgikapudgalagrahaśceti
eṣāmabhiprāyaḥ - yadi paramāṇu saṃdohātmake śarīre'ntarvayupapattyaṅgipudgalo nāsti, paramāṇusandohātmake kāye vinaṣṭe satyaparakāya- grahaṇaṃ kaḥ kariṣyati ? tasmādupapādukapudgalo'sti, tena sādhitena svargaphalaṃ nirvāṇaphalaṃ ca bhavati
svargaphalādaparaṃ nirvāṇaṃ nāma na syāditi tattvapṛcchākāle teṣāṃ svacittā- bhiprāyamabhijñāya tattvavidā bhagavatoktam - asthi puggalo bhāravāho ṇa piccaṃ bhaṇāmi ṇāṇiccaṃ bhaṇāmi iti
etadeva satyaṃ bhagavato vacanāt
svapnāvasthāyāṃ cittavāsanāpudgalo nānityo na nityo vaktuṃ śakyate
anena tathāgatavacanena mleccha- dharmaṃ tyaktvā bauddhā vaibhāṣikā jātāḥ
punarlokottaradharmaṃ deśyamānaṃ śrutvā bodhisattvānā- miha pudgalagrahaṃ parityajya kecit samyak saṃbuddhamārgamāśritā iti

eṣāmarthavādināmabhiprāyaḥ- yadyākāśakuharāntarvati sacarācaraṃ vastu nāsti tadā trailokyaṃ nāma na syāt, saṃsārā- bhāve sugatirdurgatirna syāt evaṃ buddhabodhisattvāśca na syuḥ paramanirvāṇamapi na syāt, bhagavato'pi dhātusaṃdarśanaṃ na syāt, dravyāntarvartino'rthābhāvāditi
evaṃ tattvapṛcchākāle teṣāṃ cittābhiprāyaṃ jñātvā bhagavatoktam — asti paramāṇusandohātmakaṃ caramaśarīraṃ dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇairyuktam yasya prasādena samyaksambuddhatvaṃ mahāparinirvāṇaṃ bhavati, tadeva satyam, bhagavato dhātusaṃdarśanāt

jyotiṣābhyāsa vaśenārthasaṃdarśanāt
iha hi yat kaivalyaṃ jñānaṃ tadeva jyotiṣāṅgaṃ sarvasatvasādhāraṇamupadeśavaśād gaṇitavaśājjñāyate naṣṭajātakādikam, yathā'nyaiḥ karṇapiśācikā sādhitā sā kathayati anyaiḥ kṣetrapālāḥ sādhitāste kathayanti yatkiñciccitte sphurati teṣām
anyairmātaraḥ sādhitā āveśitāḥ parakāye praviśya kathayanti, anyairnāgarājāḥ sādhitāste kāladaṣṭāḥ kāye praviśya kathayanti, anyaiśceṭakāḥ sādhitāste sādhakānāṃ vākyena sattvakāye praviśya sattvakāyaṃ viḍambayanti, anyairḍākinīrākṣasādayaḥ sādhitāste sattvānāṃ raktaṃ pibanti, anyebhūtāḥ sādhitāste sattvānāṃ kāye praviśya dhvananakampanādikaṃ kṛtvā gacchanti, anyavighna- vināyakāḥ sādhitāste sattvānāṃ vighnāni kurvanti, anyaiḥ saumyadevatāḥ sādhitāste sattvānāṃ rakṣāṃ kurvanti, anyaiḥ kāmadevatāḥ sādhitāste sattvānāṃ kāmonmādaṃ śukracyuti kurvanti, anyeranyā duṣṭadevatāḥ sādhitāḥ kecit stambhanaṃ kurvanti kecinmohanaṃ keci - duccāṭanaṃ kecidvidveṣaṇaṃ kecinmāraṇaṃ kecid vṛkṣaliṅgotpāṭanaṃ ca kurvanti, eva- mādīnyanekāni kṣudropadravakarmāṇyadṛṣṭarūpeṇa kurvanti

jñānaṃ pratyayasāraṃ kāmaviṣanirañjanānām, yasya kāmājñā nāsti nirañjana - viṣājñā nāsti nirañjana - āṇavaśāmbhavaśākteya pratyakṣaparokṣavedhājñā nāsti, sa paṇḍito'pi paśuḥ
