trilagna trināṭyāmardharātrādudayaṃ
yāvaditi | tato dīrghaṣaṇmātrā bhinnānyudayānmadhyāhnaṃ yāvadaparatrilagna trināḍhyāṃ śvāsa mātrā bhavanti pūrvasaṃkhyā iti
bhūyo hrasvaṣaṇmātrābhinnaṃ madhyāhnānniśisamaya- stasminnastaṅgataparyantaṃ trilagna trināḍyāṃ pūrvavat saṃkhyā iti
evaṃ niśāyā aparabhāge'rdha- rātraṃ yāvat trilagna trināḍyāṃ pūrvasaṃkhyā samātrā
evaṃ śvāsocchvāsān vahanti pratidina- samaye khakhaṣaṭ candranetrāniti ṣaṭśatādhikaikaviṃśatsahasrāṇi
ebhiḥ śvāsamadhye pūrvā āhṛtya śūnyāḥ samaviṣamagateryojayenmadhyamāyāmiti
iha pūrvoktāḥ pratyekamaṇḍale sapādekādaśa, te ca makarādilagno' dayabhedena pratyekamaṇḍalāntā dakārādibhedena madhya- māyāṃ yojayediti
asau mahāprapañco bahutvānna likhitaḥ, 'paramāvibuddhe ṣaṣṭisāhasri- kāyāṃ ṭīkāyāṃ jñātavya iti
na cānena loko[b]ttarakāraṇaṃ kiñcidasti, yena prayatnaḥ kartavya iti
asyādikāderlomakeśasaṃkhyaṃ yāvannirgama iti || 130 ||
idānīmaṣṭamārdhā yā sārdhasaptānāṃ parā, sā ucyate- bāhye yā cāṣṭamārdhā prabhavati ghaṭikā rāhubhogāt parasthā śvāsādhaṃ sā svadehe khalu vigatatamā sarvalokāvabhāsā
pūrvārdhā sāndhakāraṃ tribhuvanasakalaṃ līyate yatra śūnye tasmāttāṃ bhedayitvā viśati gatatamāṃ kālacakrekayogī ||131||
bāhya ityādi
iha bāhye lokadhātau yā cāṣṭamārghā mātrā prabhavati ghaṭikā rāhubhogāt parasyeti
iha pratyekartī māsadvayena rāhuḥ pañcaviṣayalakṣaṇāḥ pañcanāḍyaḥ, tathā sattvarajastamolakṣaṇāstisrastā bhuṅkte, rāśicakramadhye tāsu sārdhasapta bhuṅkte
ardhanāḍīṃ bhoktuṃ na kṣamaḥ
sā rāhubhogāt parasthā śvāsārdhaṃ sā svadehe'nutpannasya bālasya daṇḍākāraṃ nābhilalāṭayormadhye bāhye nirgamābhāvādardhaśvāsa ucyate
mantrabhedenārdhamātrā
sā khalu vigatatamā sarvalokāvabhāsā ṣāṇmāsikagarbhasya pañcābhijñātaḥ
pūrvārdhā sāndhakāraṃ tribhuvanasakalaṃ līyate yatra śūnye maraṇānte turyāṃ tyaktvā sā suṣuptāyāṃ viśataḥ, ṣaṇmāsaṃ yāvat
tataḥ svapnāvasthāṃ prāpto garbho'parārdhamātrāṃ viśati
tasmāttāṃ tamo'- ityadhikam
nāsti
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- vasthāṃ bhedayitvā hṛdaye praviśati gatatamāṃ nābhicakre sthitāṃ kālacakrekayogī prāṇa- vāhaniruddha iti garbhāvasthāṃ gata iti yogināṃ yoganiṣpattiḥ || 131 ||
idānīmutpannānāṃ bālānāṃ kalāhānyādirucyate- candronā yā'dhikārke vibhuparamakalā sā'tra sandhyācatuṣke yāme yāme vrajantī niśidivasavaśādardhaṃlagnaprabhedeḥ
ṣaṭtriṃśat kāladūtyo dviguṇanṛpatayo'nyāstithī dvayaṣṭabhede vārāṇāṃ cāṣṭabhede khalu punarapare naikasandhipradeśe || 132 ||
candronetyādi
iha yathā bāhya tathā'dhyātmani candrasya pratidinaṃ ṣaṣṭighaṭikā tithinaṃ bhavati, ekaghaṭikonā sā cādhyātmani śukrasyeti
yā'dhikā'rka iti sūryabhoge pūrvoktā'dhyātmapaṭale dve nāḍike
vibhuriti bodhicittam, tasya paramakalā jñānadharmiṇī candrakalāhāniḥ, vijñānadharmaṇī sūryabhogahāniriti
sā'tra sandhyācatuṣke yāme yāme vrajantī niśidivasavaśādaghaṃlagna prabhedaiḥ ṣa‌ṭtriṃśat kāladūtya iti tricakrasaṃvare ḍākinyaḥ
tāsāṃ kulikāyoge sañcāraḥ
sandhyābhedena praharalagnārdhabhedena
yathā bāhya bhogo layo- 'dhikāra ādhipatyam, tathā'dhyātmanyādityodayabhedena prāṇodaya bhedeneti
iha bāhye catu- dvīpeṣu yatrādityodayastatra bhogaḥ, yatrārdharātraṃ tatra layaḥ, yatra madhyāhnaṃ tatrādhikāraḥ, yatrā- stamanaṃ tatrādhipatyam
kulikāyā niśāpraveśato 'yoginībhuktaye'rdharātraṃ yāvaditi udayānmadhyāhnaṃ yāvad bhikṣūṇāṃ bhogo divākāle, tena dinastu bhagavān vajrī prajñā naktaṃ prabhuktaye iti niyamaḥ
tena udayānmadhyāhnaṃ yāvad bhikṣubhirbhuktaṃ nirāmiṣaṃ bhoktavyam
astaṅgamanādardharātraṃ yāvadyogiyoginībhiḥ samayakāryaṃ kartavyam
tadūrdhvaṃ saṃvarabhaṅgaḥ śrāvakamantriṇoḥ, tadbhaṅgādṛddhisiddhihāniriti
evaṃ yathā sandhyābhedena caturyoginīnāṃ bhogalayādikaṃ tathā śmaśānayoginīnāmaṣṭānāṃ praharabhedenārdharātrānmadhyāhnaṃ yāvadanulomena pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu sañcāraḥ, catuḥprahareṣu madhyāhlādardharātraparyantaṃ vilomena īśāna vāyavyanairṛtyāgneya dikṣu tathā lagnārdhabhedena vṛścikadhanurlagnārdhabhedena cittacakre dakṣiṇāvartena sañcāraḥ
catasṛṇāṃ makarakumbhe vāmāvartena
evaṃ vākcake mīnameṣavṛṣa- mithunārdhabhedena tathā kāyacakre karkaṭasiṃhakanyātulābhedena sañcāra iti ṣaṭtriṃśatīnāṃ sañcāraḥ prāṇabhedataḥ
tāśca kāladūtya:, tathāha cakrasaṃvare- madhyamottamaśvāsena gandhodakayutena ca
kulikāṃ pūjayennityaṃ kālaviśeṣeṇa dūtikāḥ || iti
atra paramākṣarasukhena viṇmūtracandrasūryanāḍī nirodhena kulikāṃ vajravārāhīṃ pūjayet
uktakālaviśeṣeṇa dūtikāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat pūjayet prāṇasañcāreṇa bāhye'dhyātmani ca
iha nānopāyavineyamahoddeśaḥ nābhicakre prathamaparimaṇḍale caturdalāni
teṣu pūrva dalamātmapīṭhaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ parapīṭhaṃ paścimaṃ mantrapīṭhamuttaraṃ tattvapīṭhamiti
tathā mukhadvāraṃ śūlabhedaśmaśānaṃ pūrvaṃ dakṣiṇe nāsārandhaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ śavadahanamapānadvāraṃ paścimaṃ pūtigandhaṃ vāmanāsārandhramuttaraṃ klinnam
evaṃ vāmakarṇamīśāne bālamṛtyuḥ, nairṛtye dakṣiṇakaṇaṃ ghora yuddhama, vāyavye sarpadaṣṭaṃ dakṣiṇanetram, agnau vāmanetramucchiṣṭamiti
evaṃ hṛdayacakre'ṣṭanāḍīnāṃ saṃjñā rohiṇī pūrṇagiriḥ pūrva- nāḍī piṅgalā, jālandharaṃ dakṣiṇanāḍī jayā, oḍḍiyāṇaṃ paścimanāḍī iḍā, arbuda- uttaranāḍīti dikṣu
tathā vidikṣu - īśāne kuhā godāvarī, vāyavye'lambuṣā rāmeśvarī, nairṛtye pūṣā devīkoṭam, āgneyyāṃ hastijihvā mālavakam
evaṃ kaṇṭhacakre prathamapari- maṇḍale'ṣṭanāḍyaḥ kṣetropakṣetracchando ho pacchandohasaṃjñābhijñeyāḥ
evaṃ lalāṭe'pi prathamapari- maṇḍale'ṣṭanāḍyo mela|pakopamelāpaka 'śmaśānopaśmaśānanāmabhirveditavyā iti cakrasaṃvare niyamaḥ
āsāṃ bījānyantasthā ya ra la vāḥ kāyacakre pūrvādidikṣu, tathā īśānādi- vidikṣu vā lā rāyā iti
ha hā haṃ haḥ agni vāyu-īśānanairṛtyaśmaśāneṣu
evaṃ e ar al o vākcakre dikṣu, vidikṣu au āl ār ai
a ā aṃ aḥ pūrvāparavāma- dakṣiṇaśmaśāneṣu
cittacakre i ṛ ḷ u dikṣu, vidikṣu ū ṛ ī iti
garbhapadmadale a ā pūrvāpare, aṃ a: vāmadakṣiṇe dvādaśabhujasyeti
tathā mūlatantre bhagavānāha - nābhimadhye sthitaṃ cittaṃ jñānavijñānayogataḥ
prāṇasyāṣṭaguṇairbaddhaṃ kośakīṭairivātmajaiḥ ||
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptāvasthā trijñānabhedataḥ
prāṇināṃ trimukhaṃ cittaṃ turyākālāccaturmukham ||
evaṃ vijñānayogena pañcaṣaviṣayātmakam
pañcāsyaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ cittamacittaṃ cittamanyat ( māpyate ) ||
nābhimadhye sthito bindurvisarga : saṃpuṭai stayoḥ
jñānavijñānayogena bodhicittaṃ vyavasthitam ||
sandhyāyāmadhalagneṣu prāṇasañcārabhedataḥ
ṣaṭtriṃśad dūtikā'troktā bālānāmavatāraṇe || iti
etāstāvat ṣaṭtriṃśannāḍikā : prāṇapravāhataḥ kāladūtyaḥ śarorakṣayakāriṇyaḥ
tasmāttāsu gataḥ prāṇo nirodhanoya iti nītārthaḥ
idānīṃ guhyanāḍyo bodhicittavāhinyo dvātriṃśaducyante - dviguṇanṛpataya ityādi
iha dviguṇanṛpataya iti dvātriṃśad guhyacakre jñānabhedenāvasthitāḥ, tāsāṃ ṣoḍaśarāgapakṣe śukle kalābhedena sthitāḥ, ṣoḍaśavirāgabhedena kṛṣṇapakṣe candrakalākṣayabhedena pūrvoktāḥ
tathā hevatre- abhedyā sūkṣmarūpā ca divyā vāmā tu vāmanī ityādi
ca. ' śmaśānopaśmaśāna' nāsti
( he ta dvātriṃśannāḍya ādhārabhūtā etāvatyo ḍākinya ādheyadharmiṇyo'pānavāyusaṃyo- gāditi
etābhistrayastri~śadātmako herukaḥ samājo vā bhavati, guhyatantratvāditi
tasya dviguṇaṃ nirmāṇacakre catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginyātmakaṃ cakraṃ pañcamaṇḍalapravāhakamiti
vārāṇāṃ cāṣṭabheve punarvāranāḍyo hutkamale gauryādyaṣṭau, lalāṭe ṣoḍaśa nairātmyādyā iti
evamanekasandhipradeśe ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśatasandhipradeśe nāḍyo varṣadinasaṃkhyāstāsāmādheyabhūtā yoginyaḥ
evaṃ dvāsaptatisahasrayoginīnāmādhārādheyabhedeneti
yāvatyo nāḍyastāvatyo ḍākinya: prāṇāpānacārata iti niṣprapañcavaco bhagavataḥ || 132 ||
idānīṃ bhagavato mudrocyate-
vedartuśvāsaśeṣā tribhuvanajananī ḍākinī viśvarūpā yā hīnā candramadhye paramajinapateḥ sā svamudrā dvidhā syāt
yā'rkasthā sāṣṭabhedā diśividiśidale yāmabhogāvasānā śeṣā'nyāścakradevyaḥ paramabhayakarā bāhyadehe samastāḥ || 133 ||
vedavityādi
iha vebarturiti catuḥṣaṣṭistadante yā naṣṭacandrakalā saṃsāriṇām, tadvaidharmyeṇānaṣṭacandrakalā śvāsaśeṣā catuḥṣaṣṭiśvāsānāmantimā śvāsadharmiṇī tribhuvana- jananī ḍākino viśvarūpā
yā honā candramadhye paramajinapateḥ bodhicittasya, sā svamudrā dvidhā syāt
ekā saṃvṛtidharmiṇī mahāmāyā mahāraudrā bhūtasaṃhārakāriṇī, dvitīyā mahāprajñā bodhicittasya buddhānāṃ prajñāpāramitā utpādavyaya rahitā buddhamāyā mahāsaumyā sṛṣṭisaṃhāranāśinī
sā anāśravabhedādapānasthā
yā'rkasthā sāṣṭabhevā iti prāṇasthā rajodharmaṇī, sā'ṣṭabhedā jāgratsvapna suṣuptaguṇatrayapañca viṣayabhedenāṣṭa- guṇātmiketi
sā ca maṇḍale padmāṣṭadaleṣu sthitā iti kalpanā
śeṣā anyāḥ pūrvoktāścakradevyaḥ parikalpitāḥ
tāḥ paramabhayakarā bāhyadehe samastā iti nāḍī- sañcāraniyamaḥ || 133 ||
idānīṃ grahacārabhogaḍākinya ucyante- mande randhrā dinākhyā tamini suraguroḥ sārdhanetrākṣisaṃkhyāḥ sārdhāḥ ṣaḍ vedajātāḥ paramajinapateścittava trodbhavāstāḥ
bhaume śūnyāhicandrā ravibudhabhṛguketośca śūnyā hikhekā khākṣyakṣyekāśca jātāḥ khalu paramavibhoḥ śuddhavāgudbhavāstāḥ || 134 ||
manda ityādi
iha bāhye sūryapracāreṇa māsadvayena ṛtuḥ sūryasya, adhyātmani lagnadvayena prāṇasya ṛtuḥ
sa ca vāmadakṣiṇadaśamaṇḍalapravāheṇa bhavati sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedena
tena ṛto mande mandabhoge randhrā iti navaghaṭikābhogo rāśicakre, tāśca ḍākinyaḥ
paṭale, ślo
] nānopāyavineyamahoddeśaḥ evaṃ vinākhyā iti [2092 ] pañcadaśeti 'rātrī bhoge suraguroḥ sārdhanetrākṣisaṃkhyā iti sārdhadvāviṃśaditi, ekībhūtāḥ sārdhāḥ ṣaḍ vedajātā iti sārdhaṣaṭcatvāriṃśaditi
parama jinapate citavanodbhavāstā iti cittabindujanitā ityarthaḥ
bhaume śūnyāhicandrā iti prapañcaḥ
tasya maṇḍalena yallabhyate ṣaṣṭibhāgena tāvatyo nāḍya iti niyamaḥ, sāmānyenāśītyuttaraśatam
iha yatra maṅgalasya ūno bhāgastatra ravikābhogo na hartavyaḥ, candrabhogo māsaikaḥ kṣepaṇīyaḥ
iha maṅgalasya trayoviṃśatimāseḥ svadinaṃ lokarūḍhyā aṣṭādaśamāsaiḥ, tanna ghaṭate, tenātra yathābhūtaṃ varṣaśataṃ grahāṇāṃ maṇḍala dinerbhavati, aṣṭamāsaika- viṃśatidinādhikamiti niyamaḥ
evaṃ bhaume śūnyāhicandrāḥ
ravibudhabhūguketośca śūnyāhikhaikā ityaṣṭāśītyuttarasahasram
eṣāṃ pañcagrahāṇāmekatra bhogāt khākṣyakṣye- kāśca jātā iti viṃśatyadhikadviśatādhikaikasahasram
khalu paramavibhoḥ śuddhavāgudbha- vāstā iti vāgbandujanitā ityarthaḥ || 134||
yā candrasyartubhuktiḥ khakharasaśikhinaḥ kāyavajrodbhavāstā ekībhūtāḥ samastā rasaṣaḍahiyugāścārdhanāḍīstathaiva
ḍākinyaḥ kālarūpāḥ sakalatanugatāḥ prāṇināṃ prāṇahantrya- stasmāttāḥ sādhanīyāḥ pratidinasamaye rodhayitvā svamāgam || 135 ||
yā candrasyartabhuktiḥ khakharasaśikhina iti ṣaṭśatādhikasahasratrayaṃ ghaṭikābhogaḥ, kāyavajtrodbhavāstā iti kāryābindudbhavā iti
ekībhūtāḥ samastā navānāṃ rasabaḍahi- yugā iti ṣaṭṣaṣṭyadhikāṣṭaśatādhikasahasracatuṣkam, ardhanāḍī tathaiveti saṃkhyā ḍākinīnāmekartī
tā ḍākinyaḥ kālarūpāḥ sakalatanugatā devādīnāṃ prāṇināṃ prāṇahantrya ekartī
ṣaḍṛtuṣu punarayutadvayaṃ navasahasraśatamekaṃ ṣaṇṇavatyadhikamiti ḍākinīsaṃkhyā sattvānāṃ grahacaraṇāntarbhūtā iti niyamaḥ
tasmāttāḥ sāghanīyāḥ prati- dinasamaye roṣayitvā svamāgaṃ tāsāṃ prāṇāpānanirodhena catubindunirodheneti niyamaḥ || 135 ||
yāvadbhaktirgrahāṇāṃ svaparagatigatā śvāsaniḥśvāsacārāt tāvat kiṃ kāladūtī prabhavati varadā vyāpinī yā tridhātau
dūto sūkṣmapracāro guruvacanagato bodhicitte'kṣare ca jñātavyo yogayuktervyapagata kaluṣairnānyathā rāgacittaiḥ || 136 ||
dvitīyavṛttenoktā yāvadityādinā subodhamiti || 136 ||
vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ candracaraṇe rāhupraveśa ucyate- mārtaṇḍendvoḥ padānyapyubhayagativaśāt tṛṣṇayā tāḍitāni aṅgerbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ prasati saṃ caraṇaṃ rāhuveśe ca ketuḥ
sarvāḥ sandhyāṣṭayāmāḥ pratidinasamaye prāṇināṃ dvādaśāṅgāḥ śvāsāścaikaikalagne dviguṇanavaśataprāṇavāhāṅgamuktam ||137||
mārtaṇḍetyādi
iha mārtaṇḍapadānyekādaśa rasayugaśaśinaḥ iti pūrvoktāni, indupadāni pañcaviṃśad bhūtā bhūteṣu ityādinā, anayorekatra militāni ṣaṭtriṃśad bhavanti
tānyaṣṭaguṇairguṇitānyaṣṭāśītyuttaraśatadvayaṃ bhavanti
evaṃ tṛṣṇayā tāḍitā- nyaṣṭabhiriti
aṅgeriti dvādaśalagnerbhāgalabdhāni caturviṃśaticandrapadāni bhavanti
avaśeṣaṃ pañcaviṃśatimaṃ caraṇaṃ grasati rāhupraveśe keturiti
adhyātmani mana indriyaṃ śukradhātucaraṇaṃ śūnyāṅgaṃ yaditi
sarvāḥ sandhyāścatasro'ṣṭapraharāḥ pratidinasamaye prāṇināṃ dvādaśāṅgāste dvādaśalagnānītyarthaḥ
śvāsāce kekalagne dviguṇanavaśatānī- tyaṣṭādaśaśatāni prāṇavāhāḥ, te ekāṅgamuktā' iti
evaṃ bāhye māsekenaikāṅgamaṣṭā- daśaśatadaṇḍaiḥ, māsadvayena ṛtuḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ śataiḥ
evaṃ dvidvi-aṅgaḥ ṣaḍṛtavaḥ
ṛtutrayeṇa grahaṇaṃ candrārkarāhubhogavaśāditi || 137 ||
dvidvayaṅgeṣvādyavidyā prathamadinavaśād dvādaśāṅgāni yāvat tasmādṛtvā dināṅgāni ca paranavamī pūrvavad yāni tāni
evaṃ pañcaprakārairṛturapi ca bhavet sarvato dvidvimāsād yasminnido: kalaikā vrajati nidhanatāmādimāsaḥ sa eva || 138 ||
dvidvayaṅgeṣvādidine'vidyā, dvitīye dine saṃskāraḥ evaṃ krameṇa jarāmaraṇam
punastrayodaśame dine'vidyā, caturdaśame saṃskāraḥ, evaṃ kṛṣṇanavamyāṃ jarāmaraṇam
punardaśamyāmavidyā, ekādaśyāṃ saṃskāraḥ, evaṃ krameṇa śuklaṣaṣṭhyāṃ jarāmaraṇam
punaḥ saptamyāmavidyā, aṣṭamyāṃ saṃskāraḥ, kṛṣṇatṛtīyāyāṃ jarāmaraṇam
punaścaturthyāmavidyā, pañcamyāṃ saṃskāraḥ, evaṃ krameṇāmāvāsyāṃ (syāyāṃ ) jarāmaraṇamiti pṛthivyaptejovāyvā- kāśasvabhāvena ṛtau pañca parivartā matā iti
evamekapakṣo hrasvaḥ, māso dīrghaḥ, ṛtuḥ plutaḥ
pakṣaḥ sattvadharmī, māso rajodharmī, ṛtustamodharmīti
evaṃ pañcaprakāraiḥ ṛturapi ca bhavet
sarvato dvidvimāsāt
yasmin māse indoḥ phalekā vrajati nidhanatāṃ dvayormā- sayorādimāsaḥ sa eva tṛtīya iti catuḥṣaṣṭidinaiḥ kalākṣayataḥ ||138||
paṭale, ślo
] nānopāyavineyamahoddeśaḥ ṣaṇmāsartutrayeṇa grahaṇamapi bhaved dvādaśāṅgapratītyā ṣaṣṭho'rkasyartubhedaścaraṇagativaśāccandramadhye praviṣṭaḥ
tasmin sparśāṅgamadhye grasati sacaraṇaṃ rāhurindvarkayośca oṣadhyaḥ siddhidāstā dinaniśisamaye rāhuṇā''lokitā ye || 139 ||
ṣaṇmāsartutrayeṇa grahaṇamapi bhaved dvādaśāṅgapratotyeti
iha prathama ṛtuḥ sattvadharmī, dvitīyo rajodharmī, tṛtīyastamodharmī, tena tamaḥ praveśaḥ
māso'pyevaṃ ṣaḍdhātubhedena jñāna- dhātuparyantaṃ ṣaṣṭho māsaḥ
evamarkasyartubhedaścaraṇagativaśāccandramadhye praviṣṭo'māvāsyā- yām
tasmin sparśāṅgamadhye prasati sacaraṇaṃ rāhurindvarkayośca
oṣadhyaḥ siddhivāstā dinaniśisamaya iti
sūryagrahaṇe dinasamaye candragrahaṇe niśisamaye vakṣyamāṇā iti
rāhuṇā''lokitā ye grahaṇakāle vṛkṣādayaḥ phalanti
atropadeśa :- pratimāse sūryabhoge nakṣatradvayaṃ ghaṭikekādaśa dattvā ṣaṇmāsairdvādaśa nakṣatrāṇi bhavanti ṣaṭṣaṣṭighaṭikāḥ
candrakalāṃ tridinaghaṭikonāṃ kṛtvā trayodaśa śeṣāstrayaḥ pañcāśaditi grahaṇe'māvasyā bhoge deyā, ravikā heyā vā
tato rāhubhoge pañcacatvāriṃśad ghaṭikā ūnīkṛtya śeṣanakṣatra- bhogaṃ śodhayet
yadā pūrvamukhena grāsaḥ sūryasya tadā pare pucchabhogena bhavati
evaṃ
candrasyāpi paurṇamāsyāmiti siddhaṃ grahaṇalakṣaṇam
yathā bāhya tathā'dhyātmani pratidinaṃ ṣaṣṭhe lagne veditavyaṃ yogibhiḥ
idaṃ bālānāmagamyaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate śāstradvāreṇeti || 139 ||
idānīṃ maṅgalādīnāṃ kṣetrāṇyucyante- bhaumaḥ śukro budhendū ravibudhabhṛgavo bhaumamantrya ki mandā mantrī meṣādirāśau pratidinasamaye kṣetriṇaḥ sarvakālam
śāntāvindvarkarāśī budhabhṛgukujamantryakirā śiśca puṣṭo vaśyākṛṣṭo viyoge prabhavati maraṇe kṣetriṇo'smin yadi syāt || 140 ||
bhīma ityādi
iha bhaumādayo grahā meṣādirāśī kṣetriṇaḥ
meṣe bhaumaḥ śukro vṛṣe, budho mithune, induḥ karkaṭe, raviḥ siṃhe, budhaḥ punaḥ kanyāyām, bhṛgustulāyām, bhaumo vṛścike, mantrīti bṛhaspatirdhanuṣi, ākiriti śanirmakare, manda iti punaḥ śaniḥ kumbhe'pi, mantrī mīna iti
pratidinasamaye dvādaśalagnodaye kṣetriṇaḥ sarvakālaṃ bhoktāra ityarthaḥ
iha śāntau karmaṇi, indvarkarāśīti karkaṭasiṃhe dve rāśī, budharāśī dve mithunakanye puṣṭau, śukrarāśī dve 'vaśye vṛṣatule, ākṛṣṭau kujarāśī dve meṣavṛścike, mantri rāśī dve uccāṭane dhanurmīne, śanirāśī dve māraṇe makarakumbhe iti
yadi kṣetriṇī nakṣatra- bhogavaśenāsmin svakṣetre yadi syāttadā nānyasmin kāle'nyagraheṇādhiṣṭhito rāśi - statphaladāyako bhavati pūrvoktakarmasviti niyamaḥ || 140 ||
ākṛṣṭau
vaśye
vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ bhagavato vaktrabhedena śāntyādikamucyate- śāntāvādī sitāsyaṃ sitakanakanibhaṃ pauṣṭike hastayāme pītaṃ stambhe'gniyāme kanakaravinibhaṃ mohane'bdhau ca raktam
ākṛṣṭau pañcame syād ravijaladanibhaṃ ṣaṣṭhayāme ca vaśye kṛṣṇāsyaṃ māraṇe'drau kaṣaṇasitamihoccāṭane ho niśānte || 141 ||
śāntāvityādi
iha śāntāvādau prathamaprahare udayād bhagavataḥ sandhyāsañcāravaśena śukla mukhaṃ pūrve nāyako bhavati, tena śāntau śāntiviṣaye pūrvasādhitamantradhyānaiḥ praharekeṇa phalaṃ sādhayet
sitakanakanibhaṃ hastayāma iti dvitīyaprahare bhramaṇavaśāt paścimamuttara- ma pūrve nāyako bhavati, tena miśreṇa pauṣṭikaṃ sādhayet
potaṃ paścimavaktraṃ madhyāhnāt praharamekaṃ nāyakam tenāgniyāme tṛtīyaprahare stambhaviṣaye siddhidam
caturtha prahare kanaka- ravinibhaṃ pītaraktaṃ vimiśraṃ nāyakam tena mohanaṃ kuryāt
evaṃ pañcame prahare raktamukhaṃ nāyako bhaviṣyatyākṛṣṭau
ṣaṣṭhe raktakṛṣṇaṃ miśraṃ nāyakastena vaśyaṃ sādhayet
evaṃ kṛṣṇāsyaṃ māraṇe'drāviti saptame prahare'rdharātrāviti
kaṣaṇasitamihoccāṭane'hāvi- tyantime prahare niśāntādudayaparyantamiti
evaṃ prahara bhramaṇabhedenāṣṭāsyaḥ kālacakro bhagavān yadā, tadā catuḥsandhyābhedena catuścaraṇo bhavati, ardhapraharabhedena ṣoḍaśabhuja iti siddhaḥ
evaṃ bhujabhedenāṣṭau karmāṇi divāyā mardhapraharabhedena rātrī ceti niyamaḥ
catuḥsandhyābhedena catvāri karmāṇi - pūrvasandhyāyāṃ śāntikam, madhyāhne stambhanam astaṅgate ākṛṣṭiḥ, ardharātrau māraṇamiti
atrāpyaṣṭānanasya prajñā dvibhujeti pūrvāparasandhyāca raṇadvayam, madhyāhlādardharātraṃ bhujadvayī, samastamahorātraṃ mukhamiti bhāvanāniyamaḥ karmasādhane || 141 ||
asmād vṛttādyad dvitīyaṃ tatsubodhamiti
pṛthvītoyāgnivātā na śaśiravisurā jīvabhoktāra ete eṣāṃ jīvaśca bhoktā'pyaśubhaphalavaśād manyate'haṃ ca bhuktiḥ
dṛṣṭvā duḥkhānurakto grahabhujagasurān prārthayed bhūtavṛndaṃ mokṣe yasya prasādāt prabhavati manasastaṃ na śānti karoti || 142 ||
idānīṃ jātabālasya śvāsalakṣaṇamucyate- ādiśvāso'guṇātmā trividhaguṇavaśāt so'pi yāti trisaṃkhyaṃ tithyākhyāste'pi jātā viṣayaguṇavaśātte'pi bhūyastriguṇyāḥ
jātā bhūtābdhisaṃkhyā punarapi ca catuścārabhederhatāste nāḍyardhaśvāsasaṃkhyā punarapi ghaṭikā śvāsasaṃkhyā dviguṇyāḥ || 143 ||
ādītyādi
ihotpannasya bālasya ya āviśvāsaḥ prathamo'sau aguṇātmā sattvādigandhādiguṇarahitastaddharmā pravṛttitaḥ
tatastrividhaguṇavaśāt sattvavaśād paṭale, ślo
] nānopāya vineyamahoddeśaḥ dvitīyaḥ śvāso nirgataḥ, rajovaśāt tṛtīyaḥ, tamovaśāccaturthaḥ
teṣu pūrvo'guṇo na gṛhyate
tena saha trisaṃkhyaṃ gaṇyate vyāpakatvāt
punaste satvarajastamaḥ svabhāvāḥ pratyekaṃ pañca viṣayaguṇabhedena ekaikaḥ pañcavidho bhavati, tena tithyākhyā iti pañcadaśa, te'pi bhūyastriguṇyāḥ kāyavāścittabhedena jātā bhūtābdhisaṃkhyā iti pañcacatvāriṃśat
punarapi ca tataḥ cārabhederhatāste iti candrādityayośvārā caturdhā śīghramandavaka- nirgamapadānāṃ dhanavṛddhikṣaya-ṛṇavṛddhikṣayabhedeneti
taiścaturbhirhatā nāṭyarghaśvāsa saṃkhyā iti aśītyuttaraśatasaṃkhyā, punarapi dviguṇyāḥ śvāsasaṃkhyā ghaṭikāśvāsasaṃkhyā bhavati ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatasaṃkhyā prajñopāyasvabhāvata iti
sā ekanāḍī ekamaṇḍala- vāhinī bālasya vāme dakṣiṇe vā
tato dvitīyā tṛtīyā || 143 ||
dvitryandhīṣvṛtva gāṣṭagrahadaśabhiriyaṃ vardhitā kālanāḍī tasmād vargaprabhededvividhapathi gatā kālanāḍī samantāt
trailokyaṃ pūrayantī samasukhaphaladā candrasūrya pracārād yāvad vedā hivahniḥ prabhavati niyatā kartikā śuktihastā || 144 ||
dvitryandhīṣvityādinā daśabhiriyaṃ varṣitā kālanāḍī madhyamā daśamaṇḍala- vāhinī vāme dakṣiṇe vardhitā, ahorātreṇa ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalāni yāvaditi
tasmādavadhe - gaṃprabhevaiḥ pūrvokteḥ kakārādivarge maṃkarādilagnairiti
vividhapathi gatā kālanāḍo samantāt
trailokyamiti śarīraṃ pūrayantī dvāsaptatisahasranāḍī: pūrayantī
samasukha- phaladā sā candrasūrya pracārāditi lalanārasanāpracārāt pañcamaṇḍalavāhata iti śvāsa nirgamāt kālanāḍībhedaḥ
dvitīyo bheda ādhāne garbhasya kālanāḍīvargabhedena utpādāya vardhate
tatra vargasyaikasya ekaḥ, ṣaṭcakreṣu madhyamā'vadhūtī
dvayorvargaścatasra uṣṇīṣakamalanāḍyo nābhau prathamaparimaṇḍalam
trayāṇāṃ vargo nava tā hṛdayanāḍyo'ṣṭa nābhī dvitīyaparimaṇḍalam, navamī candrajanmasthānam
caturṇāṃ vargaḥ ṣoḍaśa, tā lalāṭe nābhicakrarāśiparimaṇḍalabāhye
pañcānāṃ varga: pañcaviṃśatistāni lalāṭe candra- padāni
ṣaṇṇāṃ vargaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśatteṣāṃ madhye kaṇṭhe dvātriṃśaccatvāro maṅgalabudhabṛhaspati- śukrāṇāṃ janmasthānaṃ nābhau candrapadaparimaṇḍalabāhye
saptānāṃ varga ekonapañcāśat kaṇṭhe dvitīyaparimaṇḍale rāśipadānyaṣṭacatvāriṃśat, ekā sūryasya janmasthānaṃ nābhau ca
aṣṭānāṃ vargaścatuḥṣaṣṭirnābhicake ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalavāhinyaḥ ṣaṣṭiścatasraḥ śūnyanāḍyaḥ
navānāṃ varga ekāśītiḥ, daśānāṃ śatam
etada' śītyuttaraṇataṃ bāhyabhedena rajodharma- tvād dviguṇaṃ ṣaṣṭayuttara triśatasandhipradeśeṣu jñātavyam
evaṃ yathā kaṇṭhe tathā guhyacakre, yathā lalāṭe tathā vajñe, yathā uṣṇīṣe tathā vajramaṇyagre catvāra iti śarīravyāptiḥ
yāvadvedā hivahniriti caturviṃśatipakṣāḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatadinānīti
evaṃ kālacakra- varṣāyanakāla- ṛtu lagna pakṣa - dinabhedena nāḍībhedaḥ siddhaḥ, dvitīyasandhyā praharārdhaprahara- vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- bhedeneti niyamaḥ
evaṃ bhagavatī tadyogāt prabhavati niyatā katikā śuktihastā
iha bhagavān varṣaśuddhayā, bhagavatī pratidinaśuddhayā
divā kartikā, rātriḥ kapāla- mityarthaḥ || 144 ||
idānīṃ kavargacakrāṇyucyante - cakrāṇīṣvabdhisaṃkhyā viṣayaguṇavaśāt kāyavākcittabhede rakṣāṃ kurvanti bhartuḥ samasukhaphaladānyaṣṭadhūmādidevyaḥ
evaṃ sūryasya bhartā paramaśaśikalāliṅgito viśvarūpaḥ devī buddhāmarāṇāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇāṃ rāhucandrārkavandyaḥ || 145||
cakrāṇītyādi
iha kakārādīni vyañjanāni triṃśat, akārādayaḥ svarāḥ pañca- daśa, ete pañcacatvāriṃśat
viṣayāḥ pañca guṇāstrayastadvaśāditi pañcasvarahasvadīrgha- yuktā ṅādaya ātmabinduvisargayuktāḥ pañcadaśa
evaṃ daśārāṇi cakrāṇi triṃśat, sakāra- paryantamanyonyānuvartīni
lādyāni ṣaḍārāṣyakāraparyantānīti cakrāṇīṣva bdhisaṃkhyā viṣayaguṇavaśāt kāyavāścitta 'bhedai rakṣāṃ kurvanti bhartuḥ kālacakrasya mahāsukhasya
samasukhaphalavānyaṣṭadhūmādidevya iti
atra ṅakāracakramucyate sarvacakrāṇāṃ bāhye'-
dhyātmani lomāgre ṅa ūrdhve ṅi pūrve ṅa dakṣiṇe ṅa uttare ḍala paścimāre la vāyavye ṅa īśe ṅa nairṛtye ṅī agnī ṅā pātāle
evaṃ ghagakhakalomadhātau carmamāṃse cavargaḥ, rakte ṭavargaḥ, rase pavargaḥ, asthiṣu tavargaḥ, majjasu śavargo vilomena
evaṃ triṃśaccakrāṇi daśārāṇi majjāntāni
tataḥ ṣaḍārāṇi pūrvāre la, dakṣiṇe laḥ, uttare laṃ paścime lā, agho lā:, ūrdhve lāṃ nāḍīṣu prāṇādiṣu
evaṃ va ra ya ha iti
tathā rajasi al pūrve, alaḥ dakṣiṇe, alaṃ uttare, ālu paścime, āla: adhasi, ālaṃ mūrdhni
evaṃ o ar e a iti
śukradhātau rakṣā ḷ pūrve, ḷ: dakṣiṇe, laṃ uttare, 'lla paścime, lū: aghasi, lṛ ūrdhve
' evaṃ u ṛ i a iti lomādiśukraparyantaṃ nava dhātavaḥ
tato vijñāna- dhātuścandramadhye dhūmādibhiḥ śūnyerveṣṭita ityarthaḥ
tatra pañcaviṣayaśūnyaṃ triguṇaśūnyaṃ dhūmā- dikam, tataḥ kalābindudarśanam, tato bimbadarśanamityarthaḥ
evaṃ sūryasya bhartā paramaśaśi- kalā ṣoḍaśī, tayāliṅgitaḥ san sarvakālaṃ viśvarūpaḥ sarvākāratvāt
devoti dhātavaḥ, buddhā iti skandhāḥ
teṣāṃ kiṃviśiṣṭānām ?
amarāṇāṃ jātijarāmaraṇarahitānām, viṣaya- viṣayiṇāṃ ca rāhucandrārkāṇāmapi vandyo mahākṣarasukhaḥ kālacako bhagavāniti || 145 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ catvāri vṛttāni subodhāni
yā bhartuḥ sūkṣmarūpā varaguṇaviṣayābhyantarā bāhyamudrā ḍākinyastāstridhāto paramabhayakarāḥ krodhacittaprasūtāḥ
paṭale, ślo
] nānopāya vineyamahoddeśaḥ bharturyānīndriyāṇi trividhabhavagataṃ kāyavākcittavacaṃ tānyete vajraḍākāḥ svasvaviṣayaguṇāliṅgitā maṅgalādyāḥ || 146 ||
rūpaṃ śabdaṃ raso gandhamaparamapi tat sparśadhūmo rajaśca sattvaṃ cittaṃ krameṇa prakaṭamapi tathāliṅgitaṃ rāhuṇā ca
teṣāṃ ṣaṇmandacārād vividhagatiriyaṃ śīghravakādicārā- daṣṭānāṃ ṣaṭpade'ṣṭo khalu viṣayaguṇā saṃsthitāścakrarūpāḥ || 147||
ādiśvāso'ṣṭabhedo viṣamaguṇagataścāvṛto ḍākinībhi- dhūmādyābhiḥ samantāt trividhagativaśād bhrāmyate ṣaḍgatisthaḥ
tanmadhye kālacakraḥ sphuradamṛtakalāliṅgitaḥ śuddhakāyo yaḥ śvāsaṃ chedayitvā viśati jinatanuṃ kālacakraḥ sa eva || 148 ||
śvāsacchedāvasāne trividhagatiriyaṃ ṣaṭprakārā na cāsti bindo rodhe samantāt paramavibhusukhaṃ vartate'nantakālam
tyaktvā saṃsārasaukhyaṃ paramagurusukhaṃ yoginā bhāvanīyaṃ caryāṃ śṛṅgārarūpāṃ paramabhayakarāṃ yoginīnāmatuṣṭim || 149 ||
idānīṃ vajraśvāse grahacaraṇapraveśa ucyate- bhartuḥ śvāse samastaṃ grahagaṇacaraṇaṃ nīyate kālayogāt śvāse khaṃ khaṃ kha khākṣyagnijalanidhigate codite sarvaśūnye
etairvarṣaiśca bāhye parama vibhupade sṛṣṭisaṃhārarūpe jñātavyaṃ svasvamānaijinapaticaraṇaṃ śvāsamadhye gatistham || 150 ||
bharturityādi
iha yathā bāhya tathā'dhyātmanīti nyāyādvāhye śaktiśvāso varṣa- dharmaḥ, tairvarṣairyadā yugāntaṃ bhavati khaṃ khaṃ kha khākṣyagnijalanidhiriti dvayayutādhikatraya- ścatvāriśallakṣāṇi varṣāṇāṃ śvāsānāṃ śakteḥ
evaṃ sarveṣāṃ sattvānām
eṣāṃ śvāsānā- mante śvāsamekaṃ varṣam, bāhye lokadhātī madhyamāyāṃ śvāsamekaṃ yasmin varṣe kalenirgamaḥ kṛtayugapraveśaḥ
evaṃ caturyugairdaśamaṇḍalanāḍīpravāhaḥ śakteḥ
ekaikamaṇḍalaṃ dvātriṃśa- tsahasrādhikaṃ caturvarṣalakṣamiti
evaṃ kṛte catvāri maṇḍalāni vāmanāḍyāmākāśavāyuteja- udakānīti
tatraiva tretāyāmekamaṇḍalaṃ vāmanāḍyāṃ pṛthvīlakṣaṇam, tato madhyamāpraveśaḥ pañcatriṃśatsahasrādhikaṃ lakṣamekaṃ pañcamaṇḍalānāṃ gṛhovā madhyamākālaḥ
tato dakṣiṇe saṃcāraḥ pṛthivīmaṇḍalamabmaṇḍalaṃ tretāyām
tato dvāpare tejovāyumaṇḍalam, kalau vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ jñāna- ākāśamaṇḍalam
tata ṛtuḥ pūrṇo bhavati
tato madhyamāśvāsānāmante ekaḥ śvāso'- dhikaḥ sarvālokaṃ karoti
yadi tena śvāsamātreṇa jāgradavasthāyāṃ tiṣṭhati sa śvāsaḥ sarva- satvānāṃ nidrāvasthāyāṃ bhavati
evaṃ dvayayutādhikatrayaścatvāriṃśallakṣaśvāsenādhyātmani grahāṇāmeka ṛturbhavati
madhyamāyāṃ śūnyamaṇḍalānte jñānamaṇḍale praveśo bhavati, tena sa grāhya iti
evaṃ jñātavyaṃ svasvamānaijinapaticaraṇaṃ śvāsamadhye gatisthamiti niyamaḥ || 150 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ caturdaśavṛttāni subodhānīti
śūnye mandapraveśa: svadinagativaśājjāyate vai yugānte śūnyākāśāmba rābdhyabdhiśaśadhara dine revatībhogaśeṣaḥ
khaṃ khaṃ khaṃ khābdhinetraḥ svagatigatadinaistatra rāhupraveśaḥ khaṃ khaṃ khaṃ khartukālaiḥ svagatigata dinerdeva mantripraveśaḥ || 151 ||
khaṃ khaṃ śūnyāmbarāṣṭā hinayana divasemaṃṅgalasya praveśaḥ sūryādīnāṃ praveśaḥ kha kha kha kha nayanāgnyabdhisaṃkhyedineśca
khaṃ khaṃ khaṃ khābdhināge kaśa ragata dine statra candrapraveśa- tasmin kāle vināśaḥ prabhavati jagataḥ svasvamānena rājan ||152||
nāḍīsaṃcāra eṣa prabhavati ca tataścandrasūryodite ca eka dvitrayabdhibāṇā rasagirivasavo vardhitā guhyanāḍī
randhrākhyā vā ranāḍyo bhavati ca daśamī sūkṣmanāḍyūrdhva mārge bhūyaḥ sā vargabhinnā saguṇasaviṣayāścakranāḍyo bhavanti || 153 ||
uṣṇīṣe hṛtpradeśe śirasi śaśipade kaṇṭhadeśe'rkacāre nābhau vai cakranāḍyo yugabhujaganṛpāścandracārairabhinnāḥ
dvātriṃśattadviguṇyāḥ sakalaravipademiśriterapyabhinnā ekāśītiḥ śataṃ yat karacaraṇagatāḥ sandhināḍyo dvibhedāḥ || 154 ||
uṣṇīṣe hṛtpradeśe śaśiravicaraṇe kaṇṭhacakre'vaśeṣā ekāśītyantimā yā prakaṭadaśavidhāste grahāḥ somyaraudrāḥ
teṣāmādyantabhāgaṃ vibhucaraṇagataṃ bhujyate kālanāḍyā vārāntaṃ cāpi bhuṅkte tribhuvanajananī ḍākinī vajradehā || 155 ||
nānopāyavineyamahoddeśaḥ ādau yā śūnyarūpā viṣayaguṇagatā tatsvabhāvā babhūva dhātuskandhān praviṣṭā punarapi ca samā cakṣurādīndriyeṣu
jāgratsvapnādiviṣṭā paramasukhasamānāhate saṃpratriṣṭā sā vidyā buddhamātā kuliśapadagatā yoginā'nveṣaṇīyā || 156 ||
bhartuḥ kāya prabhāvād bhavati varatano yogināṃ divyacakṣuḥ śrotraṃ tad vākprabhāvāt parahṛdayagataṃ jñāyate tasya cittāt
prajñākāya prabhāvāt trividhabhavagatā pūrvajānusmṛtiḥ syāt prajñāyā vākprabhāvāt prabhavati khasamā sarvadā sarvagaddhiḥ || 157||
prajñājñānasvabhāvād bhavati samasukhaṃ sarvadā'nāhataṃ yad akṣṇā'dṛśyaṃ trividhamapi bhavaṃ divyacakṣuḥ prabhāvāt
divyaśrotraprabhāvād hṛdayasukharavaḥ śrūyate prāṇinokta evaṃ sparśādi sarvaṃ bhavati narapate kāyavākcittayogāt || 158 ||
yā nāḍyo'pānamadhye trividhapathagatā mūtraviṭśukravāhāḥ prāṇāttāsāṃ pracāro bhavati navavidhaścāntimo buddhavaktram
śrotre ghrāṇe ca netre dvividha iti bhavellambikāyāṃ sajihve bindāvuṣṇīṣarandhre bhavati ca daśamī mudrito'jñānināṃ yaḥ || 159 ||
prāṇāpāne niruddhe kṣubhitaśaśadharaḥ sūryabimbaṃ prayāti abje vajraprabuddhe dravati punaraso sūryabimbārciṣā vai
vijñānaṃ jñānamekībhavati ca marutā candrasūrye niruddhe evaṃ caivaṃ tathaivaṃ trividhamapi bhavennāparaṃ kiñcidasti || 160 ||
graste candrārkabimbe nabhasi na ca divā naiva rātriḥ kadācit
sā sandhyā dehamadhye'pyamṛtapadagatā yogināṃ sarvakālam
pakṣakṣīṇo yathendurvrajati samarasaṃ sūryabimbe'mbarasthaḥ prāṇāganakṣaye ve sphuṭamapi ca tanau siddhikāle saroṣaḥ || 161 ||
naṣṭe candrārkabimbe'pyubhayapathi sadā prāṇavāte niruddhe abje vajraprabuddhe drutaśaśini mahāsūryabimbe praviṣṭe

krodhā buddhāḥ sadevyaḥ khalu rasakuliśā bhūmigarbhādayaśca pratyakṣāḥ khe bhavanti pramuditahṛdayā yoginaḥ siddhikāle

svaccha : kāyo'ṇunaṣṭaḥ prabhavati khasamo lakṣaṇādyaiḥ prapūrṇa : svacchaṃ trailokyamevāraṇavirahitaṃ svapnavad bhāti viśvam

idānīṃ lokadhātūpasaṃhāra ucyate- bhūmerākṛṣya cāṇūn jala śikhipavanaṃ preṣayet toyadhātī toyād bhūvahnivāyuṃ śikhini ca śikhino bhūmitoyānilāṇum
vāyau cākṛṣya vāyoḥ kṣitijalaśikhinaṃ karmavātaśca śūnye anyaṃ kṣetraṃ gatā'nye trividhabhavavaśāt karmavātāhatā ye || 165||
bhūmerityādi
iha buddhasya bhagavato dharmadeśanā dvidhā - ekā lokasaṃvṛtyā, dvitīyā paramārthataḥ
tatra lokasaṃvṛtimāha - akṣajā dhīranākārā sākṣādvedāṇusaṃcayam
satkāśmīramatāmbhodhivaibhāṣikamataṃ matam || iti
evaṃ tīrthikāḥ sarve paramāṇusaṃcayagrāhakā iti
tena bāhye buddhakṣetrāṇāṃ loka- dhātūnāmapyutpādakṣayo veditavyaḥ
sa ca sattvānāṃ śubhāśubhaphalabhogahetorbhavati yāvat sattvarāśistāvaditi niyamaḥ
tena lokadhātūpasaṃhārakāle karmavāto'yaṃ lokadhāturyeno- tpāditaḥ pūrvaṃ buddhakṣetraṃ ca tasya dvidhā svabhāvaḥ - eko niścala ākāśeṣu nakṣatravalloka- dhātorbuddhakṣetrasya na kutraciddiśi gamanam dvitīyo bhramaṇasvabhāvo rāśicakravadākāśe
evaṃ lokadhātvantargatānāṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ niścalasvabhāvaḥ prāṇināṃ calasvabhāvaḥ
karmavāta iti vijñānadharmataḥ
evaṃ paramāṇughaṭitānāṃ śarīrāṇāmupasaṃhāraḥ tena lokadhātūpasaṃhāra- buddhakṣetropasaṃhārakāle āvartavivartalakṣaṇe utpādavyayadharmaṇi
tatra lokadhātukṣayakāle bhūme- riti bhūmerye paramāṇava udakādidhātūnāṃ saṃyogenāvasthitāḥ tena bhūmiparamāṇurāśerākṛṣya nānopāya vineyamahoddeśaḥ bhūmiparamāṇūn pṛthak pṛthak kṛtvā toyadhātau apparamāṇurāśī kṣipati
evaṃ toyadhāto- rākṛṣya vahnau kṣipati, bahnerākṛṣya vāyo kṣipati, vāyorākṛṣyākāśe pṛthak pṛthak saṃcarati
evaṃ lokadhātūpasaṃhāraḥ
sa punaḥ kaścit pātāle kālāgnirnāma devatā, sā lokadhātuṃ dahati bhasmaṃ karotīti yaḥ kālāgniḥ, sa eva paramāṇu saṃcayaḥ
aparadharmaṃ- bhakṣaṇato yaḥ paramāṇurahitaḥ, sa na kiñciddahati na ca bhakṣayatīti
evaṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ vināśe jāte sati ye vijñānadharmiṇaḥ prāṇāste'nyaṃ kṣetraṃ lokadhātuṃ gatāḥ, yat karmavātena janitaṃ teṣāmupabhogāya
evamanantāḥ karmavātā lokadhātujanakā buddhakṣetrajanakāḥ, yathā prāṇināṃ prāṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak śarīrajanakā iti sthāvarāṇāṃ vṛddhijanakā iti siddhānta: saṃvṛtau || 165 ||
idānīṃ karmavātayuktānāṃ viharaṇamucyate- buddhakṣetraṃ samastaṃ tribhuvanajanako'karmakaṃ vīkṣayitvā śuddhāṇī sarvabuddhā ubhayasamara se veṣṭite bodhisattvāḥ
pratyekaikaṃ rasāṇāvubhayakulavaśād veṣṭite vajraputrāḥ bhūmyādyāścāmbarāntāḥ sakalakulagatāścācalādyāṃ praviṣṭā || 166 ||

tato buddhakṣetraṃ samastaṃ viracayati mahāsarvasaṃsthānavāyuriti

pūrva ityādi

evaṃ guhyābjaṃ sārghadvādaśāṅguli
tata uṣṇīṣād guhyapadmāntaṃ sārdhadvāṣaṣṭatha- ṅgulikāyamānam
pādo'dho manubhiriti caturdaśāṅgulibhiḥ

evaṃ jānusandhiḥ sphikamapi caturaṅgalibhiḥ

tathā pañcaviṃśatyadhikaśataṃ buddhakāyaḥ
arṣoraḥ sārdhasūryairiti sārdhadvādaśamānaiḥ
svabhujabhujakarāḥ khākṣirājārkamātrairiti viṃśatibhirbāhuḥ, ṣoḍaśabhirupabāhuḥ, dvādaśabhiḥ karo madhya- māṅgulyantam

sārdhārkamityādi
iha kāye guhyorṇamadhye [213a ] hṛdayam, tadeva cittacakram, nābhikaṇṭhamadhyataḥ sārdhadvādaśāṅgalam, nābhikaṇṭhasīmnaḥ pañcaviṃśatyaṅgulaṃ vākcakram, tathā guhyakamalorṇāntaṃ pañcāśadaṅgulaṃ kāyacakraṃ vajrabhūmyām

|| eteṣāmiti
eṣāṃ satvānāṃ muktihetoḥ puṇyodayakāle sasutajinapatiriti bodhisattveḥ saha buddho bhagavān karmabhūmyāṃ praviśya sattvavaineyāya garbhādhānaṃ kṛtvā, parama- karuṇayā boṣimutpādayitvā mārakleśānnipātya kṣititalanilaye dharmacakraṃ pravatyaṃ kṛtvā

tasmājjāto na naṣṭastribhavamapi gataḥ śuddhakāyo jinasya sattvārthaṃ sarvadā na tyajati jinapatiḥ karmaṇā bādhyate na |

vimalaprabhāyāṃ kākṣī kāsīsagandhaṃ samagaganacalaṃ vahnitoyaṃ śilālaṃ gaurī pṛthvī gataṃ yallavaṇamudadhijaṃ saindhavaṃ kṛṣṇa collam || 201 ||
tāpyamityādi |
iha mākṣikaṃ mahārasaṃ bhūmisvabhāvam cakārānmahārasam |

tataḥ pañca lavaṇāni - gatam, sāmudram, saindhavam, kṛṣṇalavaṇam, cullikālavaṇaṃ pṛthivyādisvabhāvaṃ yathākrameṇa || 201 ||

pṛthvī śailodakaṃ syāt śaśijalamudakaṃ vahnitoyaṃ ca vahni- rvāyuḥ śūnyasvarūpā viṣajalamaparā kartarī trisvabhāvā || 202 ||
tataḥ pañca kṣārāṇi - navasāram, yavakṣāram, sarjikākṣāram, ṭaṅgaṇakṣāram, kācalavaṇakṣāraṃ yathāsaṃkhyamākāśādisvabhāvam |
tataḥ pañca toyāni - śailobakaṃ pṛthvī- svabhāvam, candrodakaṃ toyasvabhāvam, uṣṇodakaṃ vahnisvabhāvam, viṣodakaṃ vāyusvabhāvam, kartaryudakamākāśasvabhāvam |
sā ca kartarī trividhā - sparśakartarī, chāyākartarī, śabda- kartarī, sādhakacchedanāditi || 202 ||

tataḥ pañca viṣāṇi - potamustaṃ bhūṣātusvabhāvam, śaktukaṃ toyadhātusvabhāvam, śṛṅgo vahnisvabhāvam, kṛṣṇaviṣaṃ vāyusvabhāvam kālakūṭaṃ śūnyasvabhāvam |
upaviṣamaparaṃ pacaṣā veditavyamiti |

idānīṃ rasasvabhāva ucyate- pūrvaṃ dhūmasvabhāvervrajati śikhigato jāritaḥ sa krameṇa śabdenaivotplutena prabhavati sa punaḥ kampaniṣkampa eva |
vedhī śabdī plutī na prabhavati sa punaḥ sūtravedhī sakampo niṣkampaḥ kuntavedhī trividha iha punaḥ sāritaḥ sāraṇābhiḥ || 205 ||

pūrvamityādi |
iha pūrvaṃ dhūmasvabhāvai vrajati śikhigato yaḥ sa jāri[ ]ta: krameṇa śabvaṃ kṛtvā vrajati bālaḥ plutena vrajati kumārabhūtaḥ prakampena vrajati proḍhaḥ, vṛddho niṣkampo vedhānuvedhī yadā bhavati |
atra vedhī lohasya śabdī bālo na bhavati, plutī ca na bhavati, kintu patralepena mṛdvagninā kiñcit krāmayediti kumāraḥ |
evam - bālaḥ [śca] patralepena kumāro'pyandhamūṣayā |
yuvānaḥ kuntavedhena vṛddho vedhī yathecchyā ||

saḥ punastrividhasāraṇābhirvakṣyamāṇābhiḥ sārito vṛddho bhavati, sa yathepsitena lohaṃ viddhayati |
iti rasaguṇaniyamaḥ || 205 ||

baddhe syāt khecaratvaṃ maraṇamapi tathā bhakṣite nāśameti jñānābhāve rasendraḥ kṣitipatibhirayaṃ sādhanīyaḥ prayatnāt || 206 ||
divyetyādi |
iha sarvatra rasaśāstre vajravaikāntakasparśād divyauṣaghosaṃyogād raso- 'bhrakaṃ nirmukhaṃ jaratīti tena vastrābhrakadrāvakāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ, teṣāṃ sparśena viḍeneti divyau - ṣadhyo rasoṣadhyo vakṣyamāṇāḥ, tāsāṃ balena prabhavati balavān jāritaḥ sarvalohān abhra- kādīn |
ko'sāviti |

nānopāyavineyamahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ divyoṣadhīlakṣaṇamucyate- yā lepāt tāmrapatrasya harati sahasā kālikāmauṣadhīṃ tāṃ dhanyāḥ paśyanti bhūmyāṃ sakalarujaharāṃ jāraṇīṃ lohajāteḥ |
anyābhiryo'sya bandhaḥ prabhavati niyato varṣakālāntare: sa svede: bhavedekasāhasravedhāt ||208||
saṃnyāsayogairadhikarasa yā lepādityādi |
iha tāmrapatrasya yā lepamātrādagnikṣiptasya kālikāṃ sahasā tatkṣaṇāda harati, oṣadhīṃ tāṃ divyāṃ mahārasatulyāṃ dhanyāḥ paśyanti bhūmyāṃ sakala rujaharāṃ bhakṣitāṃ jāraṇīṃ lohajāte rasabandhanāya |
anyābhiroṣadhībhiryo'sya sūtakasya bandhaḥ prabhavati niyato varṣakālāntarairiti dvādaśavarṣaiḥ saḥ svedaiḥ saṃnyāsayogairadhikaraso bhaveda bandhaḥ |
ekasāhasraveṣādūrdhvaṃ vedho na bhavatīti niyamaḥ || 208 ||
sarvāsāmoṣadhīnāmatikaṭukarasaiḥ kṣāravargāmlavarga: sandhānaṃ kāñjikena prabhavati hi sadā mardanasvedanārtham |
śvetānāṃ tārakārye punarapi kusumairvāpitairbījaśuddhi- dvandvaṃ saubhāgyakācākhuhayakha ra viṣaiḥ snehaseko mṛdutve || 209 ||
rambhācitrādibhasmāśvagajanarajaleḥ saptadhā śodhitaśva sarvakṣārān kaṭūṃścāpi punā rūparasān śodhitān bhāvayettān |
bhūyo bhūyo'gnitāpaiḥ punarapi śatadhā bhāvayecchoṣayecca evaṃ śaṅkhasya cūrṇaṃ śatapuṭitamidaṃ gandhakaṃ bhāvitaṃ ca || 210 ||
lohānāṃ drāvaṇārthaṃ bhavati viḍamidaṃ sūtakasyāṣṭamāṃśa dolāsvedo'ṣṭarātraṃ rasahṛdayagataṃ drāvayed yāvadeva |
kvāthāt tīvro malaśca prabhavati balavān mardito jārito'sī eke lohe drute syādraviśaśivapuṣā rañjayet sarvalohān || 211 ||
sa vyāpī sāritaśca kramati samahatairnāgaraṅgaḥ sasikthe : evaṃ yaḥ sūtakasya pratidinaṃ kurute karmabhirvakṣyamāṇaiḥ |
bandhaṃ kālāntareṇa sphuṭagurughanatāṃ sarvadoṣapramuktaṃ tasya vyādhi samṛtyuṃ harati varatanau sarvadāridryaduḥkham ||212||
asmād vṛttādaparavṛttacatuṣṭayaṃ subodham || - ||
idānīṃ rasarakṣaṇārthaṃ jambhalapūjocyate- hastārghe hemapadme vasudalasahite kaṇikā garbhaṃmadhye vajraṃ śuddhendranīlaṃ surayamadhanade padmarāgendra kāntaḥ |
pṛṣṭhe karketaratnaṃ śikhidanukahare tvāyasaṃ tāmratāraṃ hemaṃ vāyavyapatre kanakapaṭalajā kaṇikāyāṃ ca piṣṭī || 213 ||
hastārdha ityādi |
iha hastārthe vitastimātre hemapadme'ṣṭadale kaṇikāyāṃ garbhamadhye bacnaṃ śuddha hemnā bandhayet |
pūrvapatre indranīlam, dakṣiṇapatre padmarāgam, paścime karketaratnaṃ pītam, uttare candrakāntaratnam |
atha āgneyyāṃ tīkṣṇaṃ bandhayet, nairṛtye tāmram, vāyavye svarṇam, īśāne raupyamiti |
